Finally, we demonstrate it is possible to automatically generate suitable interfaces by turning the problem around: Given a CB-graph, we use an off-the-shelf Constrained Horn Clause (CHC) solver to synthesize interfaces for every network component that together ensure the given correctness property.

</details>


### [107] [CCA Reimagined: An Exploratory Study of Large Language Models for Congestion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03857)
*Xiaoxuan Qin,Yufei Wang,Longfei Shangguan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过两阶段的仿真研究，验证了大语言模型（LLM）驱动的拥塞控制机制的可行性。结果表明，与传统算法相比，该方法能将延迟降低高达50%，而吞吐量损失微乎其微（小于0.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的拥塞控制算法在面对高度动态的网络环境时，往往难以实现最佳的适应性与通用性。研究者希望探索利用大语言模型（LLM）强大的推理与泛化能力，来设计更具自适应性和通用性的拥塞控制策略。

Method: 采用两阶段的“仿真引导”研究框架。第一阶段通过将LLM隔离在单一的拥塞避免阶段，进行去风险化研究，重点探索LLM的调用时机、输入信息的选择以及指令的构建方式。基于第一阶段获取的经验，第二阶段将LLM的应用扩展至多个拥塞控制阶段，提出了一种更通用的基于LLM的拥塞控制策略。

Result: 在静态和动态网络轨迹上的评估显示，与传统的拥塞控制算法（CCA）相比，基于LLM的解决方案能将延迟降低高达50%，而吞吐量的牺牲极小（例如小于0.3%）。特别是在高度动态的网络条件下，该方案展现了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 研究证实了LLM在自适应和通用拥塞控制领域的巨大潜力。只要赋予适当的控制自由度并配合有效的触发机制，基于LLM的策略能够实现显著的性能增益，为未来网络控制机制的设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we conduct an emulation-guided study to systematically investigate the feasibility of Large language model (LLM)-driven congestion control. The exploration is structured into two phases. The first phase derisks the whole capability where we isolate the role of LLM on a single yet crucial congestion avoidance phase so that we can safely examine when to invoke the LLM, what information to provide, and how to formulate LLM instructions. Based on the gained insights, we extend LLM's role to multiple congestion control phase and propose a more generic LLM-based congestion control policy. Our evaluation on both static and dynamic network traces demonstrates that the LLM-based solution can reduce latency by up to 50\% with only marginal throughput sacrifice (e.g., less than 0.3\%) compared to traditional CCAs. Overall, our exploration study confirms the potential of LLMs for adaptive and general congestion control, demonstrating that when granted appropriate control freedom and paired with an effective triggering mechanism, LLM-based policies achieve significant performance gains, particularly under highly dynamic network conditions.

</details>


### [108] [Reimagining RAN Automation in 6G: An Agentic AI Framework with Hierarchical Online Decision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03908)
*Md Arafat Habib,Medhat Elsayed,Majid Bavand,Pedro Enrique Iturria Rivera,Yigit Ozcan,Melike Erol-Kantarci*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向无线网络的智能体AI框架，由自然语言驱动的超级智能体协调三类代理（资源分配、应用编排、自愈），在提升网络性能的同时实现了操作意图验证和自动故障恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线网络日益复杂，需要更智能的管理框架来协调多种网络功能。传统方法缺乏统一的协调机制和人机交互接口，且难以处理性能下降事件。因此需要一种能够理解自然语言指令、协调多个智能代理、具备自愈能力的AI框架。

Method: 提出基于层次在线决策变换器（H-ODT）的超级智能体框架，协调三类代理：切片间/切片内资源分配代理、网络应用编排代理和自愈代理。引入智能体检索增强生成（RAG）模块整合异构知识源。设计双层操作员意图验证方法，在切片级和KPI级使用生成式AI时序预测器进行验证。

Result: 在切片级和系统级性能指标上，相比三个最先进的基线方法，实现了更高的吞吐量、更低的网络延迟和更高的能效。操作员意图验证准确率达88.5%，能够有效排除导致性能下降的操作意图。在遇到性能下降事件时，自愈能力可在无人工干预下恢复90%的原有性能，避免服务质量漂移。

Conclusion: 该智能体AI框架成功实现了自然语言驱动下的多代理协调，在提升网络性能的同时具备有效的人机交互和自愈能力，为未来无线网络的智能化管理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an Agentic Artificial Intelligence (AI) framework for wireless networks. The framework coordinates a pool of AI agents guided by Natural Language (NL) inputs from a human operator. At its core, the super agent is powered by a Hierarchical Online Decision Transformer (H-ODT). It orchestrates three categories of agents: (i) inter-slice, intra-slice resource allocation agents, (ii) network application orchestration agents, and (iii) self-healing agents. The orchestration takes place with the help of an Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) module that integrates knowledge from heterogeneous sources. In this proposed methodology, the super agent directly interfaces with operators and generates sequential policies to activate relevant agents. The proposed framework is evaluated against three state-of-the-art baselines, showing improved throughput, reduced network delay, and higher energy efficiency at both slice-level and system-wide performance metrics. Also, the proposed Agentic framework introduces a bi-level human operator intent validation methodology, both at the slice-level and Key Performance Indicator (KPI)-level using generative AI-based time series predictors. We could rule out performance-degrading operator intents with an accuracy of 88.5%. Lastly, while being interrupted by any performance-degrading events, the self-healing capability of Agentic AI in our framework automatically recovers 90% of its previous performance, avoiding quality-of-service drifts when there is no human involvement.

</details>


### [109] [UAV Control and Communication Enabled Low-Altitude Economy: Challenges, Resilient Architecture and Co-design Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04044)
*Tianhao Liang,Nanchi Su,Yuqi Ping,Guangyu Lei,Xinglin Chen,Longyu Zhou,Tingting Zhang,Qinyu Zhang,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对蜂窝联网无人机在低空经济中面临的覆盖盲区、干扰和频谱稀缺等挑战，提出了一种通信与控制协同设计框架，通过三层架构显著提升了系统的韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着低空经济的兴起，无人机（UAV）大规模部署在环境监测、物流和应急响应等领域。然而，现有的蜂窝网络环境存在覆盖盲区普遍、干扰不可预测以及频谱资源稀缺等严峻挑战，这与无人机对飞行稳定性的严格要求形成矛盾，亟需一种新的架构来保障系统的韧性。

Method: 提出了一种通信与控制协同设计框架，包含三层架构：1）飞行前战略规划；2）飞行中自适应动作；3）系统级资源编排。该框架旨在弥合易变的无线链路与刚性飞行稳定性之间的差距，并详细阐述了实现该协同设计的关键使能技术。

Result: 初步案例研究表明，所提出的协同设计框架显著提高了蜂窝联网无人机系统的韧性，能够有效应对覆盖盲区和干扰等问题。

Conclusion: 该协同设计框架为蜂窝联网无人机提供了稳健的架构基础，能够有效提升系统韧性，支撑智能低空网络的演进与发展。

Abstract: The emerging low-altitude economy has catalyzed the large-scale deployment of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), driving a paradigm shift in environment monitoring, logistics, and emergency response. However, operating within these environments presents notable challenges as pervasive coverage holes, unpredictable interference, and spectrum scarcity. To this end, this article present a communication and control co-design framework to enable a resilient architecture for cellular-connected UAVs. Specifically, we first characterize typical service applications and their stringent performance requirements, followed by a comprehensive analysis of the unique challenges. To bridge the gap between volatile wireless links and rigid flight stability, a three layered architecture is proposed, integrating pre-flight strategic planning, in-flight adaptive action, and system-level resource orchestration. Furthermore, we detail the key enabling technologies for communication and control co-design. Preliminary case studies are proposed to validate that the co-design framework significantly improve the resilience of cellular-connected UAV systems, providing a robust foundation for the evolution of intelligent low-altitude networks.

</details>


### [110] [Advanced Holographic Multi-Antenna Solutions for Global Non-Terrestrial Network Integration in IMT-2030 Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04149)
*Alfredo Nunez-Unda,Angelo Vera-Rivera,Nuwan Balasuriya,Ekram Hossain*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文论证了全息MIMO（HMIMO）作为非地面网络（NTN）集成到IMT-2030系统的理想方案，因其能以更少的射频链路实现波束成形，显著降低功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络需要实现天地一体化覆盖，但传统MIMO技术因每个天线单元需要独立的射频链路而导致高能耗，这在能源受限的非地面系统（如卫星）中尤为关键，因此亟需一种低功耗的替代方案。

Method: 提出利用全息MIMO（HMIMO）技术，该技术基于理论上的连续孔径，通过对表面阻抗进行受控调制来产生辐射，从而以显著减少的射频链路数量实现波束成形。同时，通过低轨（LEO）多用户通信场景下的案例研究，分析HMIMO的集成应用。

Result: 论证了HMIMO相比传统MIMO在非地面网络中具有显著优势，能够有效减少射频链路数量并降低功耗，并通过案例研究展示了其在LEO多用户通信中的可行性。

Conclusion: HMIMO是IMT-2030系统中非地面网络集成的合适候选技术，能有效解决非地面平台能源受限的问题，为6G天地一体化网络提供高效的天线解决方案。

Abstract: Sixth-generation (6G) networks are expected to provide ubiquitous connectivity across terrestrial and non-terrestrial domains. This will be possible by integrating non-terrestrial networks (NTNs) to extend coverage to underserved areas. Antennas are central to this vision, with multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) technologies receiving the most attention due to their ability to exploit spatial multiplexing to improve link capacity and reliability. However, conventional MIMO can consume significant energy, as each antenna element typically requires an independent RF chain. This limitation is particularly critical in non-terrestrial systems, where onboard energy resources are limited. Holographic MIMO (HMIMO) has emerged as a promising alternative in this context. These systems are based on theoretically continuous apertures, where radiation is generated through controlled modulation of surface impedance. This enables beamforming mechanisms with significantly fewer RF chains, reducing power consumption. In this work, we make the case for HMIMO as a suitable candidate for NTN integration within IMT-2030 systems. We discuss its advantages over conventional MIMO and present a case study of HMIMO integration in LEO-based multi-user communication.

</details>


### [111] [RELIEF: Turning Missing Modalities into Training Acceleration for Federated Learning on Heterogeneous IoT Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04243)
*Beining Wu,Zihao Ding,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出RELIEF框架，通过分区融合层LoRA投影矩阵解决联邦学习中系统-模态-数据异构性耦合问题，实现显著加速、节能和稀有模态性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网边缘设备上的联邦学习面临系统、模态和数据三重异构性的耦合问题：低成本设备配备较少传感器且计算能力较弱，导致最慢的设备产生最不完整的梯度信号。简单平均更新会稀释稀有模态信息并在缺失传感器参数上浪费计算，而现有方法孤立处理这三类异构性，未能解决其耦合关系。

Method: 提出RELIEF框架，将融合层低秩适应(LoRA)投影矩阵划分为模态对齐的列块，以此作为聚合、弹性训练和通信的统一接口。每个块仅在拥有该模态的设备群组内聚合，消除跨模态梯度干扰；服务器通过优先处理群组内部散度最高的块来分配个性化训练预算，使资源受限设备训练更少但更有影响力的参数。

Result: 在两个物联网传感器数据集(PAMAP2, MHEALTH)上的实验表明，相比FedAvg，RELIEF实现高达9.41倍加速和37%能耗降低，稀有模态F1值提升高达15.3个百分点；在双Jetson AGX Orin测试平台上的真实设备验证确认了这些结果。理论证明群组聚合消除了收敛界中的干扰，散度引导分配实现了次线性遗憾。

Conclusion: RELIEF成功解决了联邦学习中三重异构性的耦合问题，通过模态对齐的参数分区和智能资源分配，在保持模型性能的同时显著提升了训练效率和稀有模态的学习效果，为异构物联网环境下的联邦学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) over heterogeneous IoT edge devices faces coupled system-modality-data heterogeneity: the lower-cost device carries both fewer sensors and less computational power, so the slowest device (straggler) produces the most incomplete gradient signals. Naively averaging their updates dilutes rare-modality information and wastes computation on absent-sensor parameters, whereas existing methods handle the triple heterogeneity (system, modality, data) in isolation and none addresses their coupling. To resolve this issue, we propose RELIEF, a framework that partitions the fusion-layer Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) projection matrix into modality-aligned column blocks and uses this partition as a unified interface for aggregation, elastic training, and communication. Each block is aggregated only within the cohort of devices possessing that modality, which eliminates cross-modal gradient interference; the server then allocates personalized training budgets by prioritizing blocks with the highest cohort-internal divergence, so that resource-constrained devices train fewer but more impactful parameters. We prove that cohort-wise aggregation removes interference from the convergence bound and that the divergence-guided allocation achieves sublinear regret. Experiments on two IoT sensor datasets (PAMAP2, MHEALTH) under both full-parameter (CNN) and parameter-efficient (LoRA) training show that RELIEF achieves up to 9.41x speedup and 37% energy reduction over FedAvg with up to 15.3 pp rare-modality F1 gains, and real-device validation on a two-Jetson AGX Orin testbed confirms these results.

</details>


### [112] [A Family of Open Time-Series Foundation Models for the Radio Access Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04271)
*Ioannis Panitsas,Leandros Tassiulas*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TimeRAN，一个用于无线接入网（RAN）时序建模的统一多任务学习框架。通过构建包含355K时序数据的大规模预训练语料库TimeRAN DataPile，该框架利用轻量级基础模型学习可迁移表示，在异常检测、分类、预测和填补等多项任务上实现了最先进性能，并成功部署于5G测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的RAN智能化主要依赖于针对特定任务构建的专用模型，这种范式导致了模型碎片化严重、任务间知识难以共享、泛化能力差以及系统复杂性增加等问题。随着RAN向可编程和分解式基础设施演进，亟需一个统一的框架来解决这些局限性。

Method: 提出了TimeRAN框架，采用轻量级时序基础模型配合少量任务特定头（heads）进行多任务学习，从而学习可在不同任务间高效迁移的表示。为支持大规模预训练，构建并开源了目前最大的RAN分析时序数据集TimeRAN DataPile，包含超过355K条时序数据和5.6亿次测量，覆盖多种遥测源、协议层和部署场景。

Result: TimeRAN在异常检测、分类、预测和填补等全面的RAN分析任务评估中达到了最先进（SOTA）性能，且仅需极少或无需针对特定任务进行微调。在实际5G测试平台的概念验证中，证明该框架在资源受限的真实场景下能够高效运行。

Conclusion: TimeRAN成功证明了统一多任务学习框架在解决RAN智能分析中模型碎片化问题的有效性，通过预训练基础模型实现了跨任务的强泛化能力与高效部署，为构建AI原生的RAN基础设施提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: The Radio Access Network (RAN) is evolving into a programmable and disaggregated infrastructure that increasingly relies on AI-native algorithms for optimization and closed-loop control. However, current RAN intelligence is still largely built from task-specific models tailored to individual functions, resulting in model fragmentation, limited knowledge sharing across tasks, poor generalization, and increased system complexity. To address these limitations, we introduce TimeRAN, a unified multi-task learning framework for time-series modeling in the RAN. TimeRAN leverages a lightweight time-series foundation model with few task-specific heads to learn transferable representations that can be efficiently adapted across diverse tasks with limited supervision. To enable large-scale pretraining, we further curate and open-source TimeRAN DataPile, the largest time-series corpus for RAN analytics to date, comprising over 355K time series and 0.56B measurements across diverse telemetry sources, protocol layers, and deployment scenarios. We evaluate TimeRAN across a comprehensive set of RAN analytics tasks, including anomaly detection, classification, forecasting, and imputation, and show that it achieves state-of-the-art performance with minimal or no task-specific fine-tuning. Finally, we integrate TimeRAN into a proof-of-concept 5G testbed and demonstrate that it operates efficiently with limited resource requirements in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [113] [OrbitTransit: Traffic Delivery and Diffusion for Earth Observation via Satellite Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04368)
*Haoyuan Zhao,Long Chen,Yi Ching Chou,Hao Fang,Jiangchuan Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出OrbitTransit，一种结合卫星移动性传输与星间链路扩散的混合方法，有效缓解低轨卫星网络中地面站拥塞问题，显著降低能耗并提升负载均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对地观测需求激增，低轨卫星数量快速增长，导致地面站（作为网络出口）出现严重拥塞。现有基于星间链路（ISL）的方法难以解决地面站分布不均引起的流量竞争，且长距离ISL路径会引发可持续性问题。

Method: 提出OrbitTransit方法，采用"拾取-携带-卸载"（PCO）机制：利用卫星轨道运动进行数据投递，同时结合星间链路进行流量扩散以缓解PCO传输中的资源竞争。构建"轨道即节点"框架与避免竞争的投递策略，联合确定最优的PCO-ISL混合路径。

Result: 实验结果表明，相比最先进的地面站选择与路由算法，OrbitTransit将电池能耗降低47.16%，任务失败率减少1.09倍，并显著改善了地面站的负载均衡。

Conclusion: OrbitTransit通过创新性地融合卫星移动性传输与星间链路扩散，有效解决了地面站拥塞与资源竞争问题，在降低能耗、提升任务成功率与负载均衡方面表现优越，为大规模低轨卫星网络的数据传输提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: The emerging demand for Earth observation (EO) to address environmental challenges has driven unprecedented growth in its primary carrier, Low Earth Orbit satellites, in recent years. Ground stations (GSs), the egress points of these networks, are congested due to the massive volume of EO traffic, and their deployment is constrained by geographic, political, and budgetary factors. Although inter-satellite links (ISLs) can partially relieve this congestion by forwarding traffic to alternative GSs, existing ISL-based approaches can hardly address traffic contention caused by biased GS distribution and may also raise sustainability concerns due to prolonged ISL paths. In this paper, we propose OrbitTransit, a pickup-carry-offload (PCO) approach that leverages satellite mobility for data \textit{delivery} and integrates ISLs for traffic \textit{diffusion} to alleviate the resource contention inherent in PCO delivery. The proposed orbit-as-node framework and contention-avoidant delivery jointly determine the optimal hybrid PCO-ISL path, minimizing energy consumption and balancing GS traffic. Extensive experiments show that OrbitTransit reduces battery consumption by $47.16\%$, decreases task failures by $1.09\times$, and improves GS load balancing compared with state-of-the-art GS selection and routing algorithms.

</details>


### [114] [Comprehensive Analysis of Cellular Uplink Performance in a Dense Stadium Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04371)
*S. M. Haider Ali Shuvo,Hardani Ismu Nabil,Joshua Roy Palathinkal,Muhammad I. Rochman,Monisha Ghosh*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过对圣母大学橄榄球球场（容纳8万人）的实测研究发现，5G网络中高频TDD频段的上行链路性能存在严重瓶颈。尽管UE以接近最大功率发射，但传播损耗和TDD帧结构中上行时隙不足的限制，导致上行严重依赖低频FDD频段，下行则主要由高频TDD频段承载，形成明显的上下行不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代5G网络中上行链路性能仍然是关键瓶颈，UE需要在有限的发射功率与传播挑战之间取得平衡。然而，目前对于大规模高密度场景下（如大型体育场）5G上行链路的具体性能限制因素缺乏深入的实测研究与分析，特别是在高频TDD频段方面的系统性研究不足。

Method: 研究团队在圣母大学橄榄球球场进行了大规模实地测量，覆盖空载（赛前）和严重拥塞（比赛日）两种场景。使用Rohde & Schwarz QualiPoc设备采集物理层指标，系统分析高频TDD频段在频域（MCS、PRB分配）和时域（TDD帧结构中上下行时隙比例）的上行性能表现。此外，还在体育场停车场的Verizon COW部署点进行了传播条件较好环境下的对照测量。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）高频TDD频段上行在频域和时域均存在严重瓶颈；（2）UE即使接近3GPP最大功率发射，传播损耗仍导致低MCS和低PRB分配，无法实现宽带分配；（3）TDD帧中上行时隙数量显著少于下行时隙，进一步加剧上行瓶颈；（4）形成明显的上下行不对称：高频TDD承载大部分下行吞吐量，而网络严重依赖低频FDD频段进行上行传输；（5）在传播条件良好的COW部署点的额外测量表明，该限制并非完全由传播因素驱动，双工方式本身（TDD下行偏重的架构）也起到重要作用。

Conclusion: 研究结论强调了低频FDD频谱在维持上行容量方面不可或缺的作用。即便在TDD频段能达到更高或相当MCS的情况下，由于TDD架构对上行时隙的限制，FDD频段在上行仍具有性能优势。这些发现为下一代无线网络的设计提供了重要指导，指出需要在网络架构中充分考虑上下行不对称性以及低频FDD频谱的战略价值。

Abstract: Uplink performance remains a critical limitation in modern 5G networks, where UEs have to balance limited transmission power against propagation challenges. We conducted extensive measurements in the University of Notre Dame's football stadium, which has a seating capacity of 80,000 spectators, evaluating network behavior under both unloaded (pregame) and severely congested (game day) conditions, with a focus on uplink performance. Analyzing PHY-layer metrics captured via the Rohde & Schwarz QualiPoc, we show that high-frequency TDD bands in the uplink are severely bottlenecked in both the spectral and temporal domains. Despite transmitting near maximum 3GPP power limits, propagation loss inherent to high-frequency bands restricts UEs to low MCS indices and low PRB allocations, even in unloaded networks. This inability to achieve wideband allocation is further compounded by the significantly smaller number of uplink slots compared to downlink slots in TDD frames. Consequently, we observe a severe disparity between uplink and downlink: while high-frequency TDD bands carry the majority of downlink throughput, the network relies heavily on lower-frequency FDD bands for uplink. Additional measurements under favorable propagation conditions around a Verizon COW deployment located in the stadium parking lot also show that this limitation is not solely propagation-driven; rather, the duplexing scheme itself also plays a significant role. Even when TDD bands achieve higher or comparable MCS, FDD bands have a performance edge in the uplink due to the restrictive, downlink-heavy TDD architecture. These findings emphasize the indispensable role of low-frequency FDD spectrum in sustaining uplink capacity, providing insights that will help guide the design of next-generation wireless networks.

</details>


### [115] [nascTime: A Full-Stack 5G-TSN Bridge Simulation Framework with SDAP-Based QoS Mapping and IEEE 802.1AS Transparent Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04616)
*Mohamed Seliem,Utz Roedig,Cormac Sreenan,Dirk Pesch*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了nascTime，首个完整实现3GPP Release 16 5G-TSN网桥模型的开源仿真框架，解决了现有框架仅部分建模的问题，实现了完整的QoS映射和IEEE 802.1AS透明时钟行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G与IEEE 802.1时间敏感网络（TSN）的集成对于工业自动化中的灵活移动确定性通信至关重要。然而，现有仿真框架仅对该架构进行部分建模（如仅有QoS映射而无时间同步，或仅有时间同步而无数据面流量），且均未实现通过3GPP SDAP层进行每流数据无线承载（DRB）选择的完整QoS管道。

Method: 基于OMNeT 6.3、INET 4.6和Simu5G构建了开源仿真框架nascTime，实现了完整的3GPP Release 16 5G-TSN网桥模型。该框架提供了从TSN PCP到5G QFI的端到端QoS映射（通过SDAP/DRB管道），具有通过L2-in-GTP-U gPTP传输进行驻留时间校正的IEEE 802.1AS透明时钟行为，以及多端点双向流量扩展。网桥端口与INET的LayeredEthernetInterface和流式PHY集成，兼容时间感知整形和帧抢占等TSN特性。

Result: 通过一个三端点工厂场景验证了nascTime，展示了跨两个流量类别的近乎完美的数据包交付、带有驻留时间校正的正确gPTP同步以及零丢包率。

Conclusion: nascTime是首个在多端点拓扑中建模完整5G-TSN网桥数据路径的仿真框架，具备基于SDAP的QoS区分和实测IEEE 802.1AS透明时钟行为，填补了现有研究工具的空白。

Abstract: The integration of 5G with IEEE 802.1 Time-Sensitive Networking (TSN) is essential for enabling flexible and mobile deterministic communication in industrial automation. The 3GPP Release 16 specification defines a bridge architecture where the 5G system operates as a transparent TSN bridge, incorporating Network-side and Device-side TSN Translators (NW-TT, DS-TT), a TSN Application Function, and QoS mapping between TSN Priority Code Points and 5G QoS Flow Identifiers. However, existing simulation frameworks model only subsets of this architecture, either QoS mapping without time synchronization, or time synchronization without data plane traffic, and none implements the complete QoS pipeline through the 3GPP SDAP layer with per-flow Data Radio Bearer selection. We present nascTime[20], an open simulation framework built on OMNeT 6.3, INET 4.6, and Simu5G that implements the complete 3GPP Release 16 5G-TSN bridge model. The framework provides end-to-end QoS mapping from TSN PCP through to 5G QFI via the SDAP/DRB pipeline, IEEE 802.1AS transparent clock behavior with measured residence time correction through L2-in-GTP-U gPTP transport, and multi-endpoint scaling with bidirectional traffic. The bridge ports integrate with INET's LayeredEthernetInterface and streaming PHY for compatibility with TSN features including Time-Aware Shaping and frame preemption. We validate nascTime with a three-endpoint factory scenario demonstrating near-perfect packet delivery across two traffic classes, correct gPTP synchronization with residence time correction, and zero packet loss. nascTime is the first simulation framework to model the full 5G-TSN bridge data path with SDAP-based QoS differentiation and measured IEEE 802.1AS transparent clock behavior in a multi-endpoint topology.

</details>


### [116] [Analyzing Symbolic Properties for DRL Agents in Systems and Networking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04914)
*Mohammad Zangooei,Jannis Weil,Amr Rizk,Mina Tahmasbi Arashloo,Raouf Boutaba*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了diffRL框架，用于深度强化学习（DRL）智能体的符号属性验证。相比现有的点属性验证方法，符号属性能够覆盖更广的输入状态范围，并提供更全面的系统行为分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DRL验证方法主要关注点属性（围绕固定输入状态定义），其覆盖范围有限，且需要大量人工工作来识别相关的输入-输出对。为了确保DRL在系统与网络控制问题（如自适应视频流、无线资源管理和拥塞控制）中的安全部署，迫切需要一种能够覆盖更广泛输入状态范围、且能推理智能体行为的验证方法。

Method: 提出了一种符号属性的通用表述，以单调性和鲁棒性为具体示例。该方法将符号属性编码为同一策略相关执行之间的比较，并将其分解为实际可处理的子属性。通过diffRL框架，利用现有的深度神经网络（DNN）验证引擎对这些符号属性进行分析。

Result: 在三个基于DRL的控制系统（自适应视频流、无线资源管理、拥塞控制）上进行了广泛实验。结果表明，符号属性比点属性提供了更广泛的覆盖范围，能够发现非显而易见且具有操作意义的反例。研究还揭示了训练过程中属性满足度的演变、模型大小对可验证性的影响，以及不同验证后端之间的权衡与局限性。

Conclusion: 符号属性验证为DRL智能体提供了比传统点属性更全面、更高效的安全性保障，能够有效发现潜在的异常行为，推动了DRL在关键系统中的安全部署。该框架为未来DRL验证技术的发展提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has shown remarkable performance on complex control problems in systems and networking, including adaptive video streaming, wireless resource management, and congestion control. For safe deployment, however, it is critical to reason about how agents behave across the range of system states they encounter in practice. Existing verification-based methods in this domain primarily focus on point properties, defined around fixed input states, which offer limited coverage and require substantial manual effort to identify relevant input-output pairs for analysis. In this paper, we study symbolic properties, that specify expected behavior over ranges of input states, for DRL agents in systems and networking. We present a generic formulation for symbolic properties, with monotonicity and robustness as concrete examples, and show how they can be analyzed using existing DNN verification engines. Our approach encodes symbolic properties as comparisons between related executions of the same policy and decomposes them into practically tractable sub-properties. These techniques serve as practical enablers for applying existing verification tools to symbolic analysis. Using our framework, diffRL, we conduct an extensive empirical study across three DRL-based control systems, adaptive video streaming, wireless resource management, and congestion control. Through these case studies, we analyze symbolic properties over broad input ranges, examine how property satisfaction evolves during training, study the impact of model size on verifiability, and compare multiple verification backends. Our results show that symbolic properties provide substantially broader coverage than point properties and can uncover non-obvious, operationally meaningful counterexamples, while also revealing practical solver trade-offs and limitations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [FVRuleLearner: Operator-Level Reasoning Tree (OP-Tree)-Based Rules Learning for Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03245)
*Lily Jiaxin Wan,Chia-Tung Ho,Yunsheng Bai,Cunxi Yu,Deming Chen,Haoxing Ren*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出FVRuleLearner框架，通过算子级规则学习解决LLM生成SystemVerilog断言的挑战，在语法正确性上提升3.95%，功能正确性上提升31.17%，功能失败减少70.33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式验证（FV）虽能通过数学精确断言确保硬件正确性，但过程高度依赖人工。LLM在自然语言到SystemVerilog断言（NL-to-SVA）转换中因训练数据有限和FV算子内在复杂性而表现不佳，亟需更高效稳健的方法确保正确选择SVA算子。

Method: 提出基于算子推理树（OP-Tree）的算子级规则学习框架FVRuleLearner，分两阶段：训练阶段构建OP-Tree，将NL-to-SVA对齐分解为细粒度、算子感知的问题，组合正确断言的推理路径；测试阶段执行算子对齐检索，从学习到的OP-Tree中获取相关推理轨迹，为未见过的规范生成新规则。

Result: 在综合研究中，FVRuleLearner在语法正确性上平均超越最先进基线3.95%，功能正确性上超越31.17%；通过功能分类分析，在各类算子中成功减少平均70.33%的SVA功能失败，证明学习到的OP-Tree在未见过的NL-to-SVA任务中的算子规则生成有效性。

Conclusion: FVRuleLearner为形式验证领域的特定推理和规则学习建立了新范式，有效解决了LLM在SVA生成中的关键挑战。

Abstract: The remarkable reasoning and code generation capabilities of large language models (LLMs) have recently motivated increasing interest in automating formal verification (FV), a process that ensures hardware correctness through mathematically precise assertions but remains highly labor-intensive, particularly through the translation of natural language into SystemVerilog Assertions (NL-to-SVA). However, LLMs still struggle with SVA generation due to limited training data and the intrinsic complexity of FV operators. Consequently, a more efficient and robust methodology for ensuring correct SVA operator selection is essential for producing functionally correct assertions. To address these challenges, we introduce FVRuleLearner, an Operator-Level Rule (Op-Rule) learning framework built on a novel Operator Reasoning Tree (OP-Tree), which models SVA generation as structured, interpretable reasoning. FVRuleLearner operates in two complementary phases: (1) Training: it constructs OP-Tree that decomposes NL-to-SVA alignment into fine-grained, operator-aware questions, combining reasoning paths that lead to correct assertions; and (2) Testing: it performs operator-aligned retrieval to fetch relevant reasoning traces from the learned OP-Tree and generate new rules for unseen specifications. In the comprehensive studies, the proposed FVRuleLearner outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline by 3.95% in syntax correctness and by 31.17% in functional correctness on average. Moreover, FVRuleLearner successfully reduces an average of 70.33% of SVA functional failures across diverse operator categories through a functional taxonomy analysis, showing the effectiveness of applying learned OP-Tree to the Op-Rule generations for unseen NL-to-SVA tasks. These results establish FVRuleLearner as a new paradigm for domain-specific reasoning and rule learning in formal verification.

</details>


### [118] [A Review of Multiscale Thermal Modeling in Heterogeneous 3D ICs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03290)
*Baibhari Priya Barua,Md Rahatul Islam Udoy,Ahmedullah Aziz*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本综述系统阐述了先进2.5D/3D集成电路的多尺度热传输物理机制与建模分析方法，整合了从紧凑模型到物理信息机器学习等多种技术，并提出了热感知协同优化的设计指南与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着功率密度的增加和多裸芯片垂直集成，热量去除路径受限，导致结温升高、温度梯度增大和可靠性风险加剧，热行为已成为先进2.5D/3D集成电路的首要约束，亟需对相关物理机制和建模方法进行系统梳理。

Method: 文章将器件到系统的多尺度热传输统一到一个连贯框架中，系统分析并比较了紧凑热模型（CTM）、有限元/有限差分法（FEM/FDM）、格林函数与半解析技术、降阶与多保真度方法，以及物理信息机器学习（PIML）等多种建模方法的权衡。

Result: 揭示了界面主导传导、材料各向异性和强电热耦合的关键作用；明确了热边界电阻（TBR）和热界面材料（TIM）变异性的核心地位；指出了解耦电/热分析的陷阱以及对实测数据进行严格验证的必要性。

Conclusion: 提出了实用的设计指南，并规划了集成基于物理的建模、数据驱动代理模型和原位传感的前瞻性研究议程，以实现跨IC-封装-系统层级的热感知协同优化。

Abstract: Thermal behavior has become a first-order constraint in advanced 2.5D/3D integrated circuits (ICs) and heterogeneous packages. As power densities rise and multiple active dies are vertically integrated, heat removal paths become constricted, elevating junction temperatures, magnifying temperature gradients, and exacerbating reliability risks. This review synthesizes the physical mechanisms, modeling assumptions, and analysis methods that govern multiscale thermal transport in 3D ICs, with emphasis on interface-dominated conduction, material anisotropy, and strong electrothermal coupling. We unify device-to-system scales into a coherent framework, analyzing trade-offs among compact thermal models (CTMs), finite element/finite difference methods (FEM/FDM), Green's function and semi-analytical techniques, reduced-order and multi-fidelity methods, and physics-informed machine learning (PIML), while highlighting the central role of thermal boundary resistance (TBR) and variability in thermal interface materials (TIMs), the pitfalls of decoupled electrical/thermal analyses, and the need for rigorous validation against measurements. Finally, we outline practical design guidelines and a forward-looking research agenda that integrates physics-based modeling, data-driven surrogates, and in situ sensing to enable thermally aware co-optimization across the IC-package-system hierarchy.

</details>


### [119] [RAGnaroX: A Secure, Local-Hosted ChatOps Assistant Using Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03291)
*Benedikt Dornauer,Mircea-Cristian Racasan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出RAGnaroX，一个完全运行在普通硬件上的资源高效型ChatOps助手，实现了与依赖外部API方案相当的准确率，同时具备本地部署、可审计和高效的特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ChatOps助手解决方案通常依赖Azure或OpenAI等外部服务提供商，存在数据隐私、可审计性差以及硬件成本高的问题，因此迫切需要一种能够完全本地部署、资源高效且透明的替代方案。

Method: RAGnaroX采用Rust语言实现了一个完全本地部署的技术栈，其架构集成了模块化数据摄取、混合检索（hybrid retrieval）和函数调用功能。评估部分针对RAG管道，在SQuAD（单跳问答）、MultiHopRAG（多跳问答）和MLQA（跨语言问答）三个数据集上进行了基准测试。

Result: 实验结果显示，RAGnaroX在保持竞争力的准确率的同时具备优秀的资源效率。例如，在单跳问题上达到了0.90的上下文精度，平均响应时间为每请求2.5秒。

Conclusion: RAGnaroX证明了在不依赖外部提供商的情况下，仅使用普通硬件即可构建一个高效、可审计且功能完整的ChatOps助手，为隐私敏感场景提供了一种可行的解决方案。代码和演示材料已开源。

Abstract: This paper introduces RAGnaroX, a resource-efficient ChatOps assistant that operates entirely on commodity hardware. Unlike existing solutions that often rely on external providers such as Azure or OpenAI, RAGnaroX offers a fully auditable, on-premise stack implemented in Rust. Its architecture integrates modular data ingestion, hybrid retrieval, and function calling, enabling flexible yet secure deployment. Our evaluation focuses on the RAG pipeline, with benchmarks conducted on the SQuAD (single-hop QA), MultiHopRAG (multi-hop QA), and MLQA (cross-lingual QA) datasets. Results show that RAGnaroX achieves competitive accuracy while maintaining strong resource efficiency, for example, reaching 0.90 context precision on single-hop questions with an average response time of 2.5 seconds per request. A replication package containing the tool, the demonstration video (https://www.youtube.com/watch? v=cDxfuEbcoM4), and all supporting materials are available at https://github.com/genius-itea/RAGnaroX.git.

</details>


### [120] [ENEC: A Lossless AI Model Compression Method Enabling Fast Inference on Ascend NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03298)
*Jinwu Yang,Jiaan Wu,Zedong Liu,Xinyang Ma,Hairui Zhao,Yida Gu,Yuanhong Huang,Xingchen Liu,Wenjing Huang,Zheng Wei,Jing Xing,Yili Ma,Qingyi Zhang,Baoyi An,Zhongzhe Hu,Shaoteng Liu,Xia Zhu,Jiaxun Lu,Guangming Tan,Dingwen Tao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ENEC，一种专为华为昇腾NPU定制优化的无损压缩方法，通过块级定长编码和NPU特定优化技术，在压缩率和吞吐量上均优于现有NPU压缩器，相比GPU方案实现了3.43倍吞吐量提升和1.12倍压缩率提升，端到端推理加速最高达6.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型规模快速增长，在资源受限的专用AI硬件加速器（如华为昇腾NPU）上部署和推理面临严峻挑战，其中权重数据传输已成为关键性能瓶颈。现有无损压缩算法移植到昇腾NPU架构时吞吐量极低，亟需针对NPU特性进行专门优化的压缩方法。

Method: ENEC采用块级定长编码方案，并引入一系列NPU特定优化技术：带层次化折半位打包的位宽量化、向量化无分支整数变换、以及用于高效前缀和计算的依赖解耦段内扫描。这些技术充分利用NPU的并行计算能力，避免了传统算法中的串行依赖问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，ENEC在压缩率和吞吐量上均优于现有最先进的NPU压缩器。相比主流GPU方案，ENEC比DietGPU吞吐量高3.43倍，比nvCOMP压缩率高1.12倍。通过降低权重传输开销，ENEC实现了最高6.3倍的端到端推理加速。ENEC是昇腾NPU上首个性能可媲美GPU压缩器的开源权重无损压缩算法。

Conclusion: ENEC成功填补了昇腾NPU上高效无损压缩算法的空白，为大规模AI模型部署提供了有效解决方案，显著降低了权重传输开销并提升了推理性能，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: The rapid scaling of Large Language Models presents significant challenges for their deployment and inference, particularly on resource-constrained specialized AI hardware accelerators such as Huawei's Ascend NPUs, where weight data transfer has become a critical performance bottleneck. While lossless compression can preserve model accuracy and reduce data volume, existing lossless compression algorithms exhibit extremely low throughput when ported to the Ascend NPU architecture. In this paper, we propose ENEC, a novel lossless compression method specifically customized for AI model weights and optimized for Ascend Neural Processing Units. ENEC adopts a block-based fixed-length encoding scheme and incorporates a series of NPU-specific optimizations: bit-width quantization with hierarchical halving bit-packing, vectorized branch-free integer transformation, and dependency-decoupled intra-segment scan for efficient prefix-sum computation. Experimental results demonstrate that ENEC outperforms existing state-of-the-art NPU compressors in both compression ratio and throughput. Compared to leading GPU solutions, ENEC achieves a 3.43X higher throughput than DietGPU and a 1.12X better compression ratio than nvCOMP. By reducing weight transmission overhead, ENEC significantly improves end-to-end inference performance, achieving up to a 6.3X speedup. On Ascend NPUs, ENEC is the first open-source lossless compression algorithm for model weights that achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art GPU compressors, offering an effective solution for deploying large-scale AI models.

</details>


### [121] [Computer Architecture's AlphaZero Moment: Automated Discovery in an Encircled World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03312)
*Karthikeyan Sankaralingam*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 摩尔定律终结后，人工架构探索效率极低（<0.001%），需转向自动化“创意工厂”，将设计周期从数月压缩至数周。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着摩尔定律和登纳德缩放定律的终结，晶体管缩放带来的收益递减，架构创新成为提升性能的主要手段。然而，人工团队每代仅能探索50-100个设计，采样率不足0.001%。在当前资源受限的范式下，要求在相同的晶体管预算下实现2倍性能提升，传统的低效探索方式已无法满足需求。

Method: 提出自动化“创意工厂”（automated idea factories），通过多层评估流水线每周生成并评估数千个候选架构，并利用部署后的遥测数据进行持续反馈学习，实现对架构设计空间的系统性探索。

Result: 早期结果表明，该系统能将架构设计周期从两位数月缩短至个位数周，探索的候选方案数量比任何人工团队多出几个数量级，且速度大幅提升。

Conclusion: 预测在两年内，纯粹人工驱动的架构研究将像人类棋手对抗引擎一样过时，自动化系统将成为架构设计的主流。

Abstract: The end of Moore's Law and Dennard scaling has fundamentally changed the economics of computer architecture. With transistor scaling delivering diminishing returns, architectural innovation is now the primary - and perhaps only - remaining lever for performance improvement. However, we argue that human-driven architecture research is fundamentally ill-suited for this new era. The architectural design space is vast (effectively infinite for practical purposes), yet human teams explore perhaps 50-100 designs per generation, sampling less than 0.001% of possibilities. This approach worked during the abundance era when Moore's Law provided a rising tide that lifted all designs. In the current scarcity paradigm, where every architecture must deliver 2X performance improvements using essentially the same transistor budget, systematic exploration becomes critical. We propose a concrete alternative: automated idea factories that generate and evaluate thousands of candidate architectures weekly through multi-tiered evaluation pipelines, learning from deployed telemetry data in a continuous feedback loop. Early results suggest that such systems can compress architectural design cycles from double-digit months to single-digit weeks by exploring orders of magnitude more candidates than any human team, and do it much faster. We predict that within 2 years, purely human-driven architecture research will be as obsolete as human chess players competing against engines.

</details>


### [122] [InsightBoard: An Interactive Multi-Metric Visualization and Fairness Analysis Plugin for TensorBoard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03323)
*Ray Zeyao Chen,Christan Grant*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了InsightBoard，一个交互式TensorBoard插件，通过集成同步多指标可视化和基于切片的公平性诊断，解决了现有训练监控工具在子群公平性分析方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习系统在安全关键领域部署时，不仅需要关注整体性能，还需要了解训练动态如何随时间影响子群公平性。现有的训练仪表板主要支持单指标监控，在检查异构指标之间的关系或诊断训练期间的子群差异方面支持有限。

Method: 开发了InsightBoard，一个交互式TensorBoard插件，在统一界面中集成同步多指标可视化与基于切片的公平性诊断。该工具通过联动多视图图表、相关性分析以及在用户定义切片上计算的标准组公平性指标，使实践者能够联合检查训练动态、性能指标和子群差异。

Result: 通过在BDD100k数据集上使用YOLOX进行的案例研究，证明了即使在整体性能优异的模型中，仍可能存在显著的人口统计学和环境差异，这些差异在传统监控下仍然隐藏。

Conclusion: InsightBoard通过在训练期间提供公平性诊断，支持更早、更明智的模型检查，且无需修改现有训练流程或引入额外的数据存储。

Abstract: Modern machine learning systems deployed in safety-critical domains require visibility not only into aggregate performance but also into how training dynamics affect subgroup fairness over time. Existing training dashboards primarily support single-metric monitoring and offer limited support for examining relationships between heterogeneous metrics or diagnosing subgroup disparities during training. We present InsightBoard, an interactive TensorBoard plugin that integrates synchronized multi-metric visualization with slice-based fairness diagnostics in a unified interface. InsightBoard enables practitioners to jointly inspect training dynamics, performance metrics, and subgroup disparities through linked multi-view plots, correlation analysis, and standard group fairness indicators computed over user-defined slices. Through case studies with YOLOX on the BDD100k dataset, we demonstrate that models achieving strong aggregate performance can still exhibit substantial demographic and environmental disparities that remain hidden under conventional monitoring. By making fairness diagnostics available during training, InsightBoard supports earlier, more informed model inspection without modifying existing training pipelines or introducing additional data stores.

</details>


### [123] [Fast Cross-Operator Optimization of Attention Dataflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03446)
*Haodong Chang,Hailiang Hu,Zhenrui Wang,Yu Gong,Rongjian Liang,Zhexiang Tang,Bo Yuan,Jiang Hu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出MMEE方法，通过基于矩阵乘法的编码枚举技术，实现对Transformer注意力机制跨算子数据流的高效优化，相比现有方法能耗降低40%-50%，延迟降低31%-69%，搜索速度提升64-343倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制是Transformer和大语言模型的主要计算负载，其数据流优化对性能和能效至关重要。然而优化决策涉及分块、计算顺序、缓冲区管理等多个维度，且需在算子内和算子间层面进行，导致决策空间极其复杂，现有方法难以高效找到最优解。

Method: 提出MMEE（矩阵乘法编码枚举）方法，核心是构建一个跨越大型决策空间的分析性能模型，采用矩阵编码方式表示多个候选方案，通过高效剪枝技术实现快速枚举评估，从而在庞大的设计空间中穷举搜索最优数据流方案。

Result: 在能耗驱动优化场景下，MMEE相比最先进方法能耗降低48%-50%，延迟降低31%-69%；在延迟驱动优化场景下，能耗和延迟分别降低40%-50%和40%-69%；搜索速度相比先前工作快64倍至343倍。

Conclusion: MMEE证明了通过矩阵编码结合穷举枚举的方法能够高效地探索大规模设计空间，为注意力计算的数据流优化提供了更高质量的解决方案，在能效和性能方面均显著优于现有方法，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Attention is a fundamental computational kernel that accounts for the majority of the workload in transformer and LLM computing. Optimizing dataflow is crucial for enhancing both performance and energy efficiency in attention computation. This optimization involves a range of decisions, such as tiling, computation ordering and buffer management, and can be applied at both intra-operator and inter-operator levels, resulting in a highly complex decision space. We propose a new approach to cross-operator dataflow optimization. Its centerpiece is an analytical performance model that spans a large decision space and enables matrix-based encoding of multiple candidate solutions. Built on this foundation, a vast number of solutions can be evaluated rapidly, and with the aid of an effective pruning technique, the optimal solution can be identified through exhaustive enumeration. We refer to our method as MMEE (Matrix Multiplication Encoded Enumeration). The ability to efficiently enumerate a large design space allows MMEE to deliver higher-quality solutions at a substantially faster speed compared to prior approaches. The MMEE approach is evaluated across various test cases for different accelerator configurations. For energy-driven optimization, MMEE reduces energy consumption by 48%-50% and latency by 31%-69%, compared to state-of-the-art methods. For latency-driven optimization, MMEE achieves simultaneous reductions of 40%-50% in energy consumption and 40%-69% in latency, respectively. Additionally, MMEE is $64\times$ to $343\times$ faster than previous works.

</details>


### [124] [Efficient Solving for Dynamic Data Structure Constraint Satisfaction Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03624)
*Nanbing Li,Weijie Peng,Jin Luo,Shuai Wang,Yihui Li,Jun Fang,Yun Liang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对集成电路验证中动态数据结构约束求解效率低下的问题，提出了D2SCSP形式化模型及一套依赖引导的增量求解框架，在工业基准测试上相比基线平均加速24.80倍，相比先进商业仿真器加速1.72倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代集成电路的功能验证广泛采用约束随机验证，但工业环境中的约束求解常涉及在运行时才能确定形状和内容的动态数据结构，导致变量集和约束实例在求解过程中不断演变。这种演变在嵌套和高维结构反复扩展时会产生巨大的计算开销，现有方法难以高效处理此类问题。

Method: 1. 将此类问题形式化为动态数据结构约束满足问题（D2SCSP），描述动态数据结构扩展与约束评估之间的交互。\n2. 提出依赖引导的问题划分框架，结合增量编码和约束激活机制。\n3. 该机制能够在多次求解间复用求解器状态和编码，避免重复计算。\n4. 将框架集成至工业SystemVerilog验证流程，并在商业仿真器VeriSim中实现。

Result: 在工业基准测试上的实验结果表明，该方法相比基线实现平均24.80倍的加速，相比最先进的商业仿真器实现1.72倍的加速，验证了其在实际验证工作流中的高效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法有效解决了动态数据结构约束求解的性能瓶颈，显著提升了工业验证流程的效率，具有很强的实用价值，可用于实际的集成电路验证场景。

Abstract: Functional verification plays a central role in ensuring the correctness of modern integrated circuit designs, where constrained-random verification is widely adopted to generate diverse stimuli under high-level constraints. In industrial verification environments, constraint solving increasingly involves dynamic data structures whose shape and content are determined at runtime, causing the sets of variables and constraint instances to evolve across solver invocations, which in turn leads to substantial overhead when nested and high-dimensional structures repeatedly expand across solves. We formalize this class of problems as the Dynamic Data Structure Constraint Satisfaction Problem (D2SCSP),which captures the interaction between dynamic data structure expansion and constraint evaluation. We propose a dependency-guided problem partitioning framework combined with an incremental encoding and constraint activation mechanism, enabling reuse of solver state and encodings across multiple solves. The framework is integrated into an industrial SystemVerilog verification flow and implemented in the commercial simulator VeriSim. Experimental results on industrial benchmarks demonstrate significant performance improvements, achieving an average speedup of 24.80x over a baseline and 1.72x over a state-of-the-art commercial simulator, highlighting the practicality of the approach for real-world verification workflows.

</details>


### [125] [L-SPINE: A Low-Precision SIMD Spiking Neural Compute Engine for Resource-efficient Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03626)
*Sonu Kumar,Mukul Lokhande,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了L-SPINE，一种支持低精度SIMD的脉冲神经网络计算引擎，在FPGA上实现了毫秒级推理延迟，能效相比CPU/GPU提升三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNNs）虽具有能效优势，但在边缘端硬件部署面临存储开销大、缩放运算效率低以及并行度受限等瓶颈，亟需一种高效的硬件加速方案。

Method: 提出了L-SPINE架构，采用统一多精度数据通路支持2-bit、4-bit和8-bit运算；利用无乘法器的移位-加法模型实现神经元动力学和突触累积；在AMD VC707 FPGA上进行实现与验证。

Result: 单个神经元仅需459 LUTs和408 FFs，关键路径延迟0.39 ns，功耗4.2 mW；系统级实现消耗46.37K LUTs、30.4K FFs，延迟2.38 ms，功耗0.54 W；相比CPU/GPU平台，推理延迟从秒级降至毫秒级，能效提升高达三个数量级；INT2/INT4量化配置显著减少内存占用且精度损失极小。

Conclusion: L-SPINE被证明是一种可扩展且高效的解决方案，能够支持实时边缘端SNN部署，在能效、延迟和资源占用方面均表现优异。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer a promising solution for energy-efficient edge intelligence; however, their hardware deployment is constrained by memory overhead, inefficient scaling operations, and limited parallelism. This work proposes L-SPINE, a low-precision SIMD-enabled spiking neural compute engine for efficient edge inference. The architecture features a unified multi-precision datapath supporting 2-bit, 4-bit, and 8-bit operations, leveraging a multiplier-less shift-add model for neuron dynamics and synaptic accumulation. Implemented on an AMD VC707 FPGA, the proposed neuron requires only 459 LUTs and 408 FFs, achieving a critical delay of 0.39 ns and 4.2 mW power. At the system level, L-SPINE achieves 46.37K LUTs, 30.4K FFs, 2.38 ms latency, and 0.54 W power. Compared to CPU and GPU platforms, it reduces inference latency from seconds to milliseconds, achieving an up to three orders-of-magnitude improvement in energy efficiency. Quantisation analysis shows that INT2/INT4 configurations significantly reduce memory footprint with minimal accuracy loss. These results establish L-SPINE as a scalable and efficient solution for real-time edge SNN deployment.

</details>


### [126] [Mambalaya: Einsum-Based Fusion Optimizations on State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03829)
*Toluwanimi O. Odemuyiwa,John D. Owens,Joel S. Emer,Michael Pellauer*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Mambalaya，一种针对Mamba工作负载的可重构加速器，通过系统性的算子融合优化，在prefill和generation阶段分别实现了4.9倍和1.9倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba是一种新兴的复杂工作负载，具有短距离和长距离依赖、非线性运算和逐元素计算等特性，在现代硬件上无法以接近峰值速度运行。其复杂的依赖关系图使得跨完整算子级联的融合变得困难，导致大量算子间内存流量开销，限制了性能表现。

Method: 利用最近提出的Einsum级联抽象来刻画Mamba的完整计算结构，应用扩展的Einsum框架系统地探索Einsum间融合机会，生成一系列减少片外Einsum间数据流量的融合映射方案，并设计支持这些映射的Mambalaya可重构加速器架构。

Result: Mambalaya在层性能上相比MARCA实现了prefill阶段4.9倍加速和generation阶段1.9倍加速；在prefill主导场景下，相比最近的细粒度内存感知Mamba融合加速器实现了最高1.5倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过基于Einsum框架的系统性融合优化方法，Mambalaya有效解决了Mamba的算子间内存流量问题，显著提升了性能，为复杂工作负载的硬件加速设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Mamba is an emerging, complex workload with various short-range and long-range dependencies, nonlinearities, and elementwise computations that are unable to run at near-peak speeds on modern hardware. Specifically, Mamba's complex dependency graph makes fusion across its full operator cascade difficult, leaving substantial inter-operator memory traffic on the table. To address these challenges, we propose Mambalaya, a novel reconfigurable accelerator that leverages fusion to overcome the limitations of Mamba. We use the recently proposed cascade-of-Einsums abstraction to characterize Mamba's full computational structure, then apply the extended Einsum framework to systematically explore inter-Einsum fusion opportunities. This principled approach yields a series of fusion mappings that reduce off-chip inter-Einsum traffic. These mappings are supported by the underlying Mambalaya architecture. Mambalaya achieves a layer performance speedup of 4.9$\times$ for prefill and 1.9$\times$ for generation over MARCA. In prefill-dominated scenarios, it achieves up to 1.5$\times$ over a recent fine-grained, memory-aware fusion accelerator for Mamba.

</details>


### [127] [3D-Stacked NMP, LLM Decoding, Systolic Array Microarchitecture, Multi-Core Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04253)
*Chenyang Ai,Yixing Zhang,Haoran Wu,Yudong Pan,Lechuan Zhao,Wenhui OU*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向3D堆叠近存处理（NMP）的大语言模型解码计算微架构，通过采用可重构脉动阵列并复用向量核心资源，在满足低面积开销、高带宽操作和细粒度可重构性要求的同时，相比Stratum实现了平均2.91倍加速和2.40倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型解码过程因算术强度低而成为推理瓶颈，性能高度依赖内存带宽。虽然3D堆叠近存处理技术能提供更高的本地内存带宽，但这也导致许多解码算子在有限的逻辑裸片面积预算下重新变为计算受限，因此计算底层的微架构设计成为首要挑战。

Method: 首先用面积效率更高的脉动阵列替代传统基于MAC树的计算单元；其次针对解码算子的形状多样性，设计支持阵列形状和数据流双重可重构的架构；最后利用近存高带宽减少片上缓存需求的特点，将脉动阵列与现有的向量核心统一设计，复用控制逻辑和多端口缓存以实现高面积效率。此外还提出了多核调度框架。

Result: 在密集模型和MoE（混合专家）模型上，相比Stratum基准方案，该方法平均实现2.91倍的速度提升和2.40倍的能效提升。

Conclusion: 通过重新思考3D堆叠NMP的计算微架构设计，充分利用高带宽优势和现有硬件资源进行创新性整合，可以有效解决LLM解码中的计算瓶颈问题，显著提升性能和能效。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) decoding is a major inference bottleneck because its low arithmetic intensity makes performance highly sensitive to memory bandwidth. 3D-stacked near-memory processing (NMP) provides substantially higher local memory bandwidth than conventional off-chip interfaces, making it a promising substrate for decode acceleration. However, our analysis shows that this bandwidth advantage also shifts many decode operators on 3D-stacked NMP back into the compute-bound regime. Under the tight area budget of the logic die, the design of the compute substrate itself therefore becomes a first-order challenge.
  Therefore, we rethink the compute microarchitecture of prior 3D-stacked NMP designs. First, we replace prior MAC tree-based compute units with a more area-efficient systolic array, and we further observe that decode operators exhibit substantial shape diversity, making reconfigurability in both systolic array shape and dataflow essential for sustaining high utilization. Building on this insight, we continue to exploit two key opportunities: the high local memory bandwidth reduces the need for large on-chip buffers, and the existing vector core, originally designed to handle auxiliary tensor computations, already provides much of the control logic and multi-ported buffering required for fine-grained flexibility for systolic array, allowing us to unify the two structures in a highly area-efficient manner. Based on these insights, we present the first compute microarchitecture tailored to 3D-stacked NMP LLM decoding, explicitly designed to satisfy the joint requirements of low area cost, high-bandwidth operation, and fine-grained reconfigurability.
  We further propose an multi-core scheduling framework. Compared with Stratum, our design achieves an average 2.91x speedup and 2.40x higher energy efficiency across both dense and MoE models.

</details>


### [128] [DHFP-PE: Dual-Precision Hybrid Floating Point Processing Element for AI Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04507)
*Shubham Kumar,Vijay Pratap Sharma,Vaibhav Neema,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持FP8和FP4格式的全流水线双精度浮点MAC处理引擎，通过创新的位分区技术实现100%硬件利用率，在28nm工艺下实现了高达60.4%的面积缩减和86.6%的功耗节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能和边缘计算中低精度算术的快速普及，对能效高且灵活的浮点乘累加（MAC）单元产生了强烈需求，现有的设计难以同时满足低功耗、高吞吐量和硬件资源高效利用的要求。

Method: 提出了一种全流水线双精度浮点MAC处理引擎，支持FP8（E4M3, E5M2）和FP4（E2M1, E1M2）格式；采用创新的位分区技术，使单个4位单元乘法器既可作为FP8的标准4x4乘法器，也可作为两个并行的2x2乘法器处理2位操作数，在不复制逻辑的情况下实现100%硬件利用率。

Result: 在28nm工艺下实现，工作频率达1.94 GHz，面积仅为0.00396 mm²，功耗为2.13 mW；与最先进的设计相比，实现了高达60.4%的面积缩减和86.6%的功耗节省。

Conclusion: 所提出的双精度浮点MAC处理引擎通过位分区技术实现了硬件资源的高效复用，为低功耗、高吞吐量的AI工作负载提供了一种能效卓越且面积紧凑的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of low-precision arithmetic in artificial intelligence and edge computing has created a strong demand for energy-efficient and flexible floating-point multiply-accumulate (MAC) units. This paper presents a fully pipelined dual-precision floating-point MAC processing engine supporting FP8 formats (E4M3, E5M2) and FP4 formats (E2M1, E1M2), specifically optimized for low-power and high-throughput AI workloads. The proposed architecture employs a novel bit-partitioning technique that enables a single 4-bit unit multiplier to operate either as a standard 4x4 multiplier for FP8 or as two parallel 2x2 multipliers for 2-bit operands, achieving 100 percent hardware utilization without duplicating logic. Implemented in 28 nm technology, the proposed processing engine achieves an operating frequency of 1.94 GHz with an area of 0.00396 mm^2 and power consumption of 2.13 mW, resulting in up to 60.4 percent area reduction and 86.6 percent power savings compared to state-of-the-art designs.

</details>


### [129] [LOCALUT: Harnessing Capacity-Computation Tradeoffs for LUT-Based Inference in DRAM-PIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04523)
*Junguk Hong,Changmin Shin,Sukjin Kim,Si Ung Noh,Taehee Kwon,Seongyeon Park,Hanjun Kim,Youngsok Kim,Jinho Lee*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出LOCALUT，一种基于查找表(LUT)的存内计算(PIM)设计，通过LUT规范化、重排序LUT和LUT切片流式传输三项技术，在UPMEM平台上实现了1.82倍的几何平均加速，用于高效低比特量化神经网络推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查找表(LUT)作为一种替代计算机制，可以将输入操作数映射到预计算结果，从而消除对算术逻辑的需求。LUT不仅降低了逻辑复杂度，还能自然支持多种数值精度，这在量化深度神经网络中日益重要。在DRAM-PIM架构中，内存容量可以替代逻辑来增加计算吞吐量，这创造了容量-计算的权衡机会。

Method: 提出三项核心技术：1）LUT规范化，通过消除重复条目来减少LUT大小；2）重排序LUT，一种轻量级辅助LUT，通过简单的LUT查找将权重向量重新映射到规范形式；3）LUT切片流式传输，一种新颖的执行策略，利用DRAM缓冲区层次结构，仅将相关的LUT列流式传输到缓冲区并在多个权重向量间重用。

Result: 在基于UPMEM设备的真实系统上评估，在各种数值精度和DNN模型上实现了1.82倍的几何平均加速。证明了所提出方法在减少LUT大小和提高计算效率方面的有效性。

Conclusion: LOCALUT为面向LUT-based DNN推理的可扩展、低逻辑PIM设计开辟了新路径，成功实现了内存容量与计算性能之间的有效权衡，为量化神经网络的高效推理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Lookup tables (LUTs) have recently gained attention as an alternative compute mechanism that maps input operands to precomputed results, eliminating the need for arithmetic logic. LUTs not only reduce logic complexity, but also naturally support diverse numerical precisions without requiring separate circuits for each bitwidth-an increasingly important feature in quantized DNNs. This creates a favorable tradeoff in PIM: memory capacity can be used in place of logic to increase computational throughput, aligning well with DRAM-PIM architectures that offer high bandwidth and easily available memory but limited logic density. In this work, we explore this capacity-computation tradeoff in LUT-based PIM designs, where memory capacity is traded for performance by packing multiple MAC operations into a single LUT lookup. Building on this insight, we propose LOCALUT, a PIM-based design for efficient low-bit quantized DNN inference using operation-packed LUTs. First, we observe that these LUTs contain extensive redundancy and introduce LUT canonicalization, which eliminates duplicate entries to reduce LUT size. Second, we propose reordering LUT, a lightweight auxiliary LUT that remaps weight vectors to their canonical form required by LUT canonicalization with a simple LUT lookup. Third, we propose LUT slice streaming, a novel execution strategy that exploits the DRAM-buffer hierarchy by streaming only relevant LUT columns into the buffer and reusing them across multiple weight vectors. Evaluated on a real system based on UPMEM devices, we demonstrate a geometric mean speedup of 1.82x across various numeric precisions and DNN models. We believe LOCALUT opens a path toward scalable, low-logic PIM designs tailored for LUT-based DNN inference. Our implementation of LOCALUT is available at https://github.com/AIS-SNU/LoCaLUT.

</details>


### [130] [Mestra: Exploring Migration on Virtualized CGRAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04694)
*Agamemnon Kyriazis,Panagiotis Miliadis,Dimitris Theodoropoulos,Nectarios Koziris,Dionisios Pnevmatikatos*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Mestra，一个支持动态调度和资源分配的CGRA多租户端到端系统，通过支持无状态和有状态的实时内核迁移来解决碎片化问题，在Alveo-U280上的实验表明，多租户空间共享可将工作负载完工时间缩短高达70.48%，实时内核迁移可将尾部延迟降低高达29.60%，且硬件开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着粗粒度可重构阵列（CGRAs）规模扩大，单应用难以高效利用硬件资源。现有映射器存在利用率低或适应性差的问题，而现有的多租户方案未能解决核心挑战：架构碎片化和实时迁移。因此，需要一种能动态调度、资源分配并有效解决碎片化问题的多租户系统。

Method: 提出Mestra系统，实现CGRA多租户的端到端解决方案。核心方法包括：1) 支持动态调度和资源分配的共享环境；2) 引入无状态和有状态两种实时内核迁移机制作为碎片整理手段，解决内核乱序完成导致的架构碎片化问题；3) 设计紧耦合控制器和回读路径以支持虚拟化和有状态迁移。在Alveo-U280数据中心级FPGA卡上进行实现和评估。

Result: 在Alveo-U280上使用PolyBench基准测试套件和常见机器学习算子内核进行评估。结果显示：1) 多用户空间共享将工作负载完工时间（makespan）提升高达70.48%；2) 实时内核迁移在碎片化布局中将尾部延迟降低高达29.60%；3) 虚拟化和有状态迁移所需的定制紧耦合控制器和回读路径仅引入每个区域0.13%的LUT开销。

Conclusion: 多租户机制对高效CGRA利用率至关重要，实时内核迁移能以极小的硬件成本回收碎片化空间并进一步提升性能。该工作证明了多租户CGRA系统的可行性和有效性，为大规模CGRAs的资源管理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: As modern Coarse Grain Reconfigurable Arrays (CGRAs) grow in size, efficient utilization of the available fabric by a single application becomes increasingly difficult. Existing CGRA mappers either fail to utilize the available fabric or rely on rigid static code transformations with limited adaptability. Multi-tenant CGRAs have emerged as a promising solution to increase hardware utilization, but current attempts fail to address key challenges such as fabric fragmentation and live migration. To address this gap, we present Mestra, an end-to-end system for CGRA multi-tenancy that supports dynamic scheduling and resource allocation in a shared environment. Mestra addresses fabric fragmentation caused by kernels completing out of order by supporting both stateless and stateful live kernel migration as a de-fragmentation mechanism. We assess our solution on an Alveo-U280 data-center-grade FPGA card, reporting area, frequency, and power. Performance is evaluated using routines from the PolyBench benchmark suite and kernels derived from common machine learning operators. Results show that spatial sharing of the available fabric across multiple users improves workload makespan by up to 70.48%, while live kernel migration reduces tail latency on fragmented layouts by up to 29.60%. The custom tightly coupled controller and read-back paths required for virtualization and stateful migration introduce a LUT cost of 0.13% per region. Our evaluation reveals that multi-tenancy is important for efficient CGRA utilization, and live kernel migration can further improve performance by recovering fragmented space with minimal hardware cost.

</details>


### [131] [Neuromorphic Computing for Low-Power Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04727)
*Keshava Katti,Pratik Chaudhari,Deep Jariwala*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文综述了经典计算面临的能效瓶颈，指出神经形态计算通过跨层协同设计（材料、器件、电路、架构和算法）为突破CMOS技术限制、提升AI系统能效和可扩展性提供了有前景的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典计算遭遇基本能效限制，传统的增加电路密度或改进半导体工艺等策略已无法解决问题，而人工智能日益增长的算力和存储需求迫切需要在信息表示、存储、通信和处理方面进行颠覆性创新。

Method: 综述经典CMOS技术的关键局限性，探讨神经形态计算方法，包括利用新型器件模态、存内计算（CIM）、模拟动力学和大脑启发的稀疏通信，并强调跨层协同设计的重要性，涵盖新材料、非易失性器件结构、新型混合信号电路与架构以及针对物理基底定制的各种学习算法。

Result: 明确了经典CMOS技术的主要限制，指出通过材料、器件、电路、架构和算法的跨层神经形态协同设计方法，有望克服这些限制并显著提升当前AI系统的能效和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 实现神经形态计算的潜力不是简单的芯片替换，而需要从材料、器件、电路、架构到算法的全栈协同设计努力，这为突破经典计算的能效瓶颈提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Classical computing is beginning to encounter fundamental limits of energy efficiency. This presents a challenge that can no longer be solved by strategies such as increasing circuit density or refining standard semiconductor processes. The growing computational and memory demands of artificial intelligence (AI) require disruptive innovation in how information is represented, stored, communicated, and processed. By leveraging novel device modalities and compute-in-memory (CIM), in addition to analog dynamics and sparse communication inspired by the brain, neuromorphic computing offers a promising path toward improvements in the energy efficiency and scalability of current AI systems. But realizing this potential is not a matter of replacing one chip with another; rather, it requires a co-design effort, spanning new materials and non-volatile device structures, novel mixed-signal circuits and architectures, and learning algorithms tailored to the physics of these substrates. This article surveys the key limitations of classical complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor (CMOS) technology and outlines how such cross-layer neuromorphic approaches may overcome them.

</details>


### [132] [DeepStack: Scalable and Accurate Design Space Exploration for Distributed 3D-Stacked AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04750)
*Zhiwen Mo,Guoyu Li,Hao,Chen,Yu Cheng,Zhengju Tang,Qianzhou Wang,Lei Wang,Shuang Liang,Lingxiao Ma,Xianqi Zhou,Yuxiao Guo,Wayne Luk,Jilong Xue,Hongxiang Fan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了DeepStack，一个面向分布式3D堆叠AI系统的性能建模工具，实现了高达10万倍的仿真加速和9.5倍的吞吐量提升，揭示了软硬件协同设计的紧密耦合关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型(LLM)扩展至数千亿甚至万亿参数，跨多个3D芯片的分布式推理变得至关重要。混合键合和封装技术的进步推动了具有更高内存带宽和容量的3D DRAM堆叠加速器的发展，亟需跨堆叠协同设计能力来进行早期系统-硬件协同设计空间探索。

Method: 在硬件层面，捕获细粒度3D内存语义，包括事务感知带宽、存储体激活约束、缓冲限制和热功耗建模；在系统层面，整合全面的并行化策略和分布式LLM推理执行调度。采用双阶段网络抽象和瓦片级计算-通信重叠等新颖建模技术，通过分层设计空间搜索探索跨越3D堆叠DRAM层、DRAM垂直连接、互连、计算-内存分配和分布式调度的设计点。

Result: 相比最先进的模拟器实现高达100,000倍的运行时加速，同时保持相当精度：与内部3D设计、NS-3后端(2.12%误差)和8xB200 GPU上的vLLM服务(12.18%误差)交叉验证。在2.5×10^14个设计点的高效探索中，通过协同优化的并行性和3D架构搜索，相比基准设计实现高达9.5倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 批次大小比预填充/解码区别驱动更根本的架构划分；并行性策略与硬件架构紧密耦合，不完整的调度搜索会导致永久次优的硅片设计，无法通过软件调优恢复。计划开源DeepStack以支持未来研究。

Abstract: Advances in hybrid bonding and packaging have driven growing interest in 3D DRAM-stacked accelerators with higher memory bandwidth and capacity. As LLMs scale to hundreds of billions or trillions of parameters, distributed inference across multiple 3D chips becomes essential. With cross-stack co-design increasingly critical, we propose DeepStack, an accurate and efficient performance model and tool to enable early-stage system-hardware co-design space exploration (DSE) for distributed 3D-stacked AI systems. At the hardware level, DeepStack captures fine-grained 3D memory semantics such as transaction-aware bandwidth, bank activation constraints, buffering limitations, and thermal-power modeling. At the system level, DeepStack incorporates comprehensive parallelization strategies and execution scheduling for distributed LLM inference. With novel modeling techniques such as dual-stage network abstraction and tile-level compute-communication overlap, we achieve up to 100,000x faster runtime over state-of-the-art simulators at comparable accuracy, cross-validated against our in-house 3D designs, NS-3 backend (2.12%), and vLLM serving on 8xB200 GPUs (12.18%). With hierarchical design space search, DeepStack enables efficient exploration over 2.5x10^14 design points spanning 3D-stacked DRAM layers, DRAM vertical connectivity, interconnect, compute-memory allocation, and distributed scheduling. Compared with baseline designs, DeepStack achieves up to 9.5x higher throughput through co-optimized parallelism and 3D architecture search. Our DSE further reveals that batch size drives a more fundamental architectural divide than the prefill/decode distinction, and that parallelism strategy and hardware architecture are tightly coupled -- incomplete schedule search leads to permanently suboptimal silicon irrecoverable by software tuning. We intend to open source DeepStack to support future research.

</details>


### [133] [A comparative study on power delivery aspects of compute-in/near-memory approaches using DRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04773)
*Siddhartha Raman Sundara Raman,Siyuan Ma,Lizy Kurian John*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文综述了DRAM-based PIM系统中的电源传输网络（PDN）挑战，提出了一个统一的分类法来表征PIM引起的电流行为，并分析了相关的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRAM-based PIM虽然具有高密度和兼容性优势，但其引入的非传统电流需求模式（如多行激活、大规摸并行执行）给电源传输网络带来了电压跌落、IR压降和热热点等严峻挑战，威胁系统的可靠性与可扩展性。

Method: 提出了一个统一的分类法，从时间（突发vs持续）和空间（局部vs分布式）两个维度表征PIM引起的电流行为；并在此框架下分析了代表性PIM技术如何通过突发激活、多行并发等方式对PDN施加压力。

Result: 揭示了PIM操作导致的电压跌落、IR压降和热热点问题的具体成因；总结了利用时序约束、内存控制器调度、数据放置以及存储体和Vault级电源管理等架构与电路级机制的DRAM特定缓解策略。

Conclusion: 强调了PDN感知设计对于构建可扩展且可靠的DRAM-based PIM系统至关重要，并为未来的研究方向提供了指引。

Abstract: Compute-in-memory (PIM) mitigates the memory wall by performing computation within memory, reducing data movement and improving energy efficiency. DRAM-based PIM is particularly attractive due to its high density, mature manufacturing ecosystem, and compatibility with existing systems. Recent works exploit multiple levels of the DRAM hierarchy - including subarrays, banks, and 3D-stacked organizations - to enable in-memory computation using mechanisms such as multi-row activation, row-buffer operations, and near-bank compute units. However, these approaches introduce non-traditional current demand patterns that challenge the power delivery network (PDN).
  This paper surveys PDN challenges in DRAM-based PIM systems and proposes a unified taxonomy that characterizes PIM-induced current behavior along temporal (burst vs. sustained) and spatial (localized vs. distributed) dimensions. Using this framework, we analyze how representative PIM techniques stress the PDN through bursty activations, multi-row concurrency, and large-scale parallel execution, leading to voltage droop, IR drop, and thermal hotspots.
  We further discuss DRAM-specific mitigation strategies leveraging existing architectural and circuit-level mechanisms, including timing constraints, memory controller scheduling, data placement, and bank- and vault-level power management. This survey highlights the importance of PDN-aware design for scalable and reliable DRAM-based PIM systems and outlines key future research directions.

</details>


### [134] [Direct Integer Division in RNS and its Hardware Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04796)
*Eric B. Olsen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文改进了RNS直接除法算法，提出基于素数幂次方的RNS系统和三种分解方法，简化了硬件结构并提升了可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差数系统(RNS)虽然提供了高效的模运算和自然并行性，但直接整数除法仍然是困难且相对不发达的操作，需要改进以提高硬件实现效率。

Method: 基于Szabo和Tanaka的II型除法算法进行改进；提出基于素数幂次方的RNS系统，增加动态范围和位效率；形式化三种分解方法：基于模的多因子缩放、用于基扩展和比较的混合基数转换、新的除数分解方法。

Result: 提出的基于素数幂次方的RNS系统增加了动态范围，提高了位效率，为除法运算提供了更大的缩放灵活性；三种分解方法简化了算法的硬件结构并提高了可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了直接RNS除法的理论和实践实现，为RNS除法的硬件实现提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Residue Number Systems (RNS) offer efficient modular arithmetic and natural parallelism, but direct integer division in RNS remains a difficult and comparatively underdeveloped operation. This paper builds on the type-II division algorithm of Szabo and Tanaka and reformulates it for more efficient hardware implementation. A principal contribution is the introduction of a power-based RNS, in which moduli are selected as powers of natural primes, increasing dynamic range, improving bit efficiency, and providing greater flexibility for scaling during division. The paper further formalizes three decomposition methods required by the division process: multi-factor scaling for modulus-based division, mixed-radix conversion for base extension and comparison, and a new divisor decomposition method introduced in this work. Each method is supported by mathematical development, including analysis of modulus invalidation during computation. These results simplify the hardware structure of the algorithm and improve its scalability. Supported by hardware diagrams and performance tables, the work advances both the theory and practical implementation of direct RNS division.

</details>


### [135] [From Concept to Practice: an Automated LLM-aided UVM Machine for RTL Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19959)
*Junhao Ye,Yuchen Hu,Ke Xu,Dingrong Pan,Qichun Chen,Jie Zhou,Shuai Zhao,Xinwei Fang,Xi Wang,Nan Guan,Zhe Jiang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出UVM^2框架，利用大语言模型自动生成UVM测试平台并通过覆盖率反馈迭代优化，显著减少手动工作量，在代码和功能覆盖率上优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IC验证是开发瓶颈，占近70%的工作量。虽然UVM提高了效率，但构建测试平台和生成足够激励仍具挑战，需要大量手动编码、重复执行EDA工具及深厚领域知识。

Method: 提出UVM^2自动化验证框架，利用大语言模型生成UVM测试平台，并基于覆盖率反馈进行迭代优化，构建了包含高达1.6K行代码RTL设计的基准测试套件。

Result: UVM^2相比经验丰富的工程师显著减少了测试平台搭建时间，平均代码覆盖率达87.44%，功能覆盖率达89.58%，分别比最先进方案提升20.96%和23.51%。

Conclusion: UVM^2通过LLM驱动的自动化方法有效解决了UVM测试平台构建的挑战，在保证验证标准的同时大幅降低了人工成本，为IC验证提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Verification presents a major bottleneck in Integrated Circuit (IC) development, consuming nearly 70% of the total development effort. While the Universal Verification Methodology (UVM) is widely used in industry to improve verification efficiency through structured and reusable testbenches, constructing these testbenches and generating sufficient stimuli remain challenging. These challenges arise from the considerable manual coding effort required, repetitive manual execution of multiple EDA tools, and the need for in-depth domain expertise to navigate complex designs.Here, we present UVM^2, an automated verification framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate UVM testbenches and iteratively refine them using coverage feedback, significantly reducing manual effort while maintaining rigorous verification standards.To evaluate UVM^2, we introduce a benchmark suite comprising Register Transfer Level (RTL) designs of up to 1.6K lines of code.The results show that UVM^2 reduces testbench setup time by up to UVM^2 compared to experienced engineers, and achieve average code and function coverage of 87.44% and 89.58%, outperforming state-of-the-art solutions by 20.96% and 23.51%, respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Integrating Artificial Intelligence, Physics, and Internet of Things: A Framework for Cultural Heritage Conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03233)
*Carmine Valentino,Federico Pichi,Francesco Colace,Dajana Conte,Gianluigi Rozza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合物联网、人工智能（特别是物理信息神经网络）和降阶方法的框架，用于文化遗产的监测与预测性维护，实现了数据驱动与物理模型的有效融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产保护亟需将技术创新与领域专业知识相结合，以实现有效的监测和预测性维护。现有方法难以同时处理复杂的3D几何模型、融合物理知识与数据驱动方法，并在保证计算效率的同时模拟环境参数影响下的退化过程。

Method: 提出一个四层功能架构的框架：1) 利用物理信息神经网络将物理定律融入深度学习模型；2) 集成降阶方法（特别是本征正交分解POD）提高计算效率；3) 兼容经典有限元方法；4) 开发工具自动管理和处理3D数字副本以直接用于仿真。框架可处理直接和反问题。

Result: 在复杂和真实几何结构的模拟场景上进行了可重现的开放实验，验证了框架各关键组件的有效性。成功实现了：对文化遗产3D模型的可靠仿真处理；PINNs在文化遗产保护中的成功应用；PINNs与ROMs的高效集成以模拟受环境和材料参数影响的退化过程。

Conclusion: 该框架为文化遗产保护提供了一种创新的技术解决方案，通过融合数据驱动与物理知识的方法，实现了对文化遗产退化过程的高效、可靠建模。框架具有开放性、可重现性，代码已开源，为未来文化遗产数字化保护工作提供了重要的技术基础和实践参考。

Abstract: The conservation of cultural heritage increasingly relies on integrating technological innovation with domain expertise to ensure effective monitoring and predictive maintenance. This paper presents a novel framework to support the preservation of cultural assets, combining Internet of Things (IoT) and Artificial Intelligence (AI) technologies, enhanced with the physical knowledge of phenomena. The framework is structured into four functional layers that permit the analysis of 3D models of cultural assets and elaborate simulations based on the knowledge acquired from data and physics. A central component of the proposed framework consists of Scientific Machine Learning, particularly Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), which incorporate physical laws into deep learning models. To enhance computational efficiency, the framework also integrates Reduced Order Methods (ROMs), specifically Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD), and is also compatible with classical Finite Element (FE) methods. Additionally, it includes tools to automatically manage and process 3D digital replicas, enabling their direct use in simulations. The proposed approach offers three main contributions: a methodology for processing 3D models of cultural assets for reliable simulation; the application of PINNs to combine data-driven and physics-based approaches in cultural heritage conservation; and the integration of PINNs with ROMs to efficiently model degradation processes influenced by environmental and material parameters. The reproducible and open-access experimental phase exploits simulated scenarios on complex and real-life geometries to test the efficacy of the proposed framework in each of its key components, allowing the possibility of dealing with both direct and inverse problems. Code availability: https://github.com/valc89/PhysicsInformedCulturalHeritage

</details>


### [137] [Scaling DPPs for RAG: Density Meets Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03240)
*Xun Sun,Baiheng Xie,Li Huang,Qiang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ScalDPP，一种面向RAG的多样性感知检索机制，通过轻量级P-Adapter引入行列式点过程（DPP），实现了可扩展的文档块间依赖建模与互补上下文选择，并结合新型集合级损失函数DML，有效解决了标准RAG检索结果冗余、信息密度不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG流程通过查询与文档块的点对点相关性排序来构建上下文，忽略了检索候选之间的相互作用。这导致检索结果存在冗余，稀释了信息密度，且无法呈现互补证据。作者认为有效的检索应联合优化密度与多样性，确保检索到的证据既信息密集又覆盖面广。

Method: 提出了ScalDPP框架，通过轻量级P-Adapter将行列式点过程（DPP）融入检索机制，实现了可扩展的文档块间依赖关系建模与互补上下文选择。此外，开发了新型集合级目标函数Diverse Margin Loss (DML)，在DPP几何空间中强制真实互补证据链优于同等规模的冗余替代方案。

Result: 实验结果证明了ScalDPP方法的优越性，验证了作者的核心观点：通过联合优化信息密度和多样性，可以有效提升RAG系统的检索质量和生成效果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过引入多样性感知检索机制，可以有效解决传统RAG系统中检索结果冗余和互补性不足的问题，为构建高质量的检索增强生成系统提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances Large Language Models (LLMs) by grounding generation in external knowledge, yielding relevance responses that are aligned with factual evidence and evolving corpora. Standard RAG pipelines construct context through relevance ranking, performing point-wise scoring between the user query and each corpora chunk. This formulation, however, ignores interactions among retrieved candidates, leading to redundant contexts that dilute density and fail to surface complementary evidence. We argue that effective retrieval should optimize jointly for both density and diversity, ensuring the grounding evidence that is dense in information yet diverse in coverage. In this study, we propose ScalDPP, a diversity-aware retrieval mechanism for RAG that incorporates Determinantal Point Processes (DPPs) through a lightweight P-Adapter, enabling scalable modeling of inter-chunk dependencies and complementary context selection. In addition, we develop a novel set-level objective, Diverse Margin Loss (DML), that enforces ground-truth complementary evidence chains to dominate any equally sized redundant alternatives under DPP geometry. Experimental results demonstrate the superiority of ScalDPP, substantiating our core statement in practice.

</details>


### [138] [DRAFT: Task Decoupled Latent Reasoning for Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03242)
*Lin Wang,Junfeng Fang,Dan Zhang,Fei Shen,Xiang Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DRAFT框架，通过将安全判断解耦为提取器和推理器两个阶段，在潜在空间中进行证据聚合，解决了工具使用LLM代理长交互轨迹中风险证据稀疏的安全监测问题，平均准确率从63.27%提升至91.18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用LLM代理的出现使安全监测从输出审核转变为审计长且嘈杂的交互轨迹，其中风险关键证据稀疏，导致标准二元监督不适合进行信用分配。

Method: 提出DRAFT框架，将安全判断解耦为两个可训练阶段：提取器将完整轨迹蒸馏为紧凑的连续潜在草稿；推理器联合关注草稿和原始轨迹以预测安全性。通过在潜在空间进行证据聚合，避免有损的显式先总结后判断流程，实现端到端可微分训练。

Result: 在ASSEBench和R-Judge等基准测试中，DRAFT持续优于强基线，平均准确率从63.27%（LoRA）提升至91.18%，并学习到更具可分性的表示。消融实验证明了提取器和推理器之间的协同效应。

Conclusion: 连续潜在推理是在长上下文监督和稀疏证据条件下实现鲁棒代理安全的实用路径。

Abstract: The advent of tool-using LLM agents shifts safety monitoring from output moderation to auditing long, noisy interaction trajectories, where risk-critical evidence is sparse-making standard binary supervision poorly suited for credit assignment. To address this, we propose DRAFT (Task Decoupled Latent Reasoning for Agent Safety), a latent reasoning framework that decouples safety judgment into two trainable stages: an Extractor that distills the full trajectory into a compact continuous latent draft, and a Reasoner that jointly attends to the draft and the original trajectory to predict safety. DRAFT avoids lossy explicit summarize-then-judge pipelines by performing evidence aggregation in latent space, enabling end-to-end differentiable training.Across benchmarks including ASSEBench and R-Judge, DRAFT consistently outperforms strong baselines, improving accuracy from 63.27% (LoRA) to 91.18% averaged over benchmarks, and learns more separable representations. Ablations demonstrate a clear synergy between the Extractor and the Reasoner.Overall, DRAFT suggests that continuous latent reasoning prior to readout is a practical path to robust agent safety under long-context supervision with sparse evidence.

</details>


### [139] [General Explicit Network (GEN): A novel deep learning architecture for solving partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03321)
*Genwei Ma,Ting Luo,Ping Yang,Xing Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种显式通用网络（GEN），将传统的点对点求解模式转变为点对函数求解模式，从而显著提升了物理信息神经网络求解偏微分方程的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理信息神经网络（PINNs）及其变体在求解偏微分方程时存在明显局限：它们主要采用离散的点对点拟合方式，忽视了真实解的潜在性质；同时，连续激活函数的使用虽能匹配局部特征，却导致模型的可扩展性和鲁棒性较差，限制了这些方法在实际应用中的成功部署。

Method: 提出一种通用显式网络（GEN），实现了从点对点到点对函数的PDE求解范式转变。该方法的核心在于利用对原始PDE的先验知识，通过构建相应的基函数来进行拟合，从而在求解过程中融入解的函数特性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够获得具有高鲁棒性和强可扩展性的解，有效克服了传统PINN方法的局限性。

Conclusion: 通过引入点对函数的求解模式并结合基于先验知识的基函数拟合，GEN方法成功解决了传统PINN方法在鲁棒性和可扩展性方面的不足，为机器学习在实际PDE求解中的更广泛应用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine learning, especially physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) and their neural network variants, has been widely used to solve problems involving partial differential equations (PDEs). The successful deployment of such methods beyond academic research remains limited. For example, PINN methods primarily consider discrete point-to-point fitting and fail to account for the potential properties of real solutions. The adoption of continuous activation functions in these approaches leads to local characteristics that align with the equation solutions while resulting in poor extensibility and robustness. A general explicit network (GEN) that implements point-to-function PDE solving is proposed in this paper. The "function" component can be constructed based on our prior knowledge of the original PDEs through corresponding basis functions for fitting. The experimental results demonstrate that this approach enables solutions with high robustness and strong extensibility to be obtained.

</details>


### [140] [Apparent Age Estimation: Challenges and Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03335)
*Justin Rainier Go,Lorenz Bernard Marqueses,Mikaella Kaye Martinez,John Kevin Patrick Sarmiento,Abien Fred Agarap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了年龄估计模型（如MVL和AMRL）在准确性与人口统计学公平性之间的权衡，发现尽管AMRL精度最高，但对亚裔和非裔美国人群体存在显著性能偏差，强调仅靠技术改进不足以解决问题，需结合多样化数据集和严格的公平性验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的外观年龄估计模型虽然在商业个性化中具有重要价值，但经常表现出人口统计学偏差。作者旨在通过回顾和评估DEX方法及其改进版本（MVL和AMRL），探究现有方法在准确性和公平性方面的表现，并揭示技术改进的局限性。

Method: 在IMDB-WIKI、APPA-REAL和FairFace三个数据集上，对比评估了采用分布学习技术（如Mean-Variance Loss和Adaptive Mean-Residue Loss）的DEX方法。研究结合了UMAP嵌入聚类分析和显著图（Saliency Maps）特征关注度分析，考察模型在不同人口统计学群体中的表现。

Result: 实验表明，AMRL方法实现了最先进的准确性，但在精确度与人口统计学公平性之间存在持续权衡。尽管UMAP嵌入显示出清晰的年龄聚类，显著图分析揭示了不同人口群体间特征关注的不一致性，导致亚裔和非裔美国人群体出现显著的性能下降。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论：仅靠技术改进不足以解决问题；准确且公平的外观年龄估计需要整合本地化和多样化的数据集，并严格遵守公平性验证协议，从而实现技术与社会责任的平衡。

Abstract: Apparent age estimation is a valuable tool for business personalization, yet current models frequently exhibit demographic biases. We review prior works on the DEX method by applying distribution learning techniques such as Mean-Variance Loss (MVL) and Adaptive Mean-Residue Loss (AMRL), and evaluate them in both accuracy and fairness. Using IMDB-WIKI, APPA-REAL, and FairFace, we demonstrate that while AMRL achieves state-of-the-art accuracy, trade-offs between precision and demographic equity persist. Despite clear age clustering in UMAP embeddings, our saliency maps indicate inconsistent feature focus across demographics, leading to significant performance degradation for Asian and African American populations. We argue that technical improvements alone are insufficient; accurate and fair apparent age estimation requires the integration of localized and diverse datasets, and strict adherence to fairness validation protocols.

</details>


### [141] [NativeTernary: A Self-Delimiting Binary Encoding with Unary Run-Length Hierarchy Markers for Ternary Neural Network Weights, Structured Data, and General Computing Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03336)
*Maharshi Savdhariya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了NativeTernary，一种针对三元权重{-1, 0, +1}的原生二进制编码方案，利用一元游程编码高效表示语义层级深度，为三元原生通用计算基础设施奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BitNet b1.58证明了大型语言模型可完全基于三元权重运行，但缺乏针对该类模型的原生二进制格式。本文旨在填补这一空白，设计一种高效、鲁棒且适用于通用计算基础设施的编码方案。

Method: 提出NativeTernary编码方案，将2比特对空间划分为三个数据符号和一个保留的结构分隔符。核心方法是利用一元游程编码表示语义层级深度：N个连续分隔符对表示层级N的边界（如字符、词、句子、段落、主题），编码成本分别为2、4、6、8、10比特，与边界稀有度成正比。分隔符可选{11}（便于OR门检测）或{00}（超低功耗CMOS优化）。提出了三种编码变体：主方案、双起始符变体及无符号与平衡三元映射分析。解码器为一个10行的无状态状态机。

Result: 设计了一套完整且灵活的编码方案，涵盖了四种比特对选择及三种变体。方案实现了高效的语义层级编码，且解码器简洁、对码流损坏具有鲁棒性。论文还阐述了在无需硬件变更的情况下，构建三元原生通用计算基础设施的路径。

Conclusion: NativeTernary为三元神经网络权重存储、分层自然语言编码及边缘计算、IoT、医疗设备等多个领域的应用提供了统一、高效且鲁棒的底层编码基础，具有广泛的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: BitNet b1.58 (Ma et al., 2024) demonstrates that large language models can operate entirely on ternary weights {-1, 0, +1}, yet no native binary wire format exists for such models. NativeTernary closes this gap. We present NativeTernary, a binary encoding scheme that partitions the 2-bit pair space into three data symbols representing ternary values -- either balanced {-1, 0, +1} or unsigned {0, 1, 2} -- and a reserved structural delimiter.
