Result: 仅改变生成器接口即可在KL正则化结果奖励后训练中创造指数级性能差距；一旦具备前缀控制能力，更丰富的观察方式可超越top-1访问。

Conclusion: 生成器访问接口的设计对自回归后训练具有决定性影响，弱前缀控制能突破根启动机制的根本限制，为算法设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: We study how generator access constrains autoregressive post-training. The central question is whether the learner is confined to fresh root-start rollouts or can return to previously built prefixes and query the next-token rule there. In the root-start regime, output sampling, generated-token log probabilities, top-$k$ reports, and full next-token distributions along sampled trajectories all reduce to one canonical experiment, limited by the on-policy probability of reaching informative prefixes. Weak prefix control breaks this barrier, and once control is available, richer observations such as conditional sampling or logits can outperform top-$1$ access. Changing only the generator interface creates an exponential gap for KL-regularized outcome-reward post-training.

</details>


### [249] [FairLogue: A Toolkit for Intersectional Fairness Analysis in Clinical Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04858)
*Nick Souligne,Vignesh Subbian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Fairlogue工具包，用于评估临床机器学习中的交叉公平性，发现交叉分析比单一轴分析揭示更大的公平性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的算法公平性工具主要关注单一人口统计学轴的比较，可能忽略了影响交叉群体的复合差异，因此需要一种能够操作性评估交叉公平性的工具。

Method: Fairlogue是一个基于Python的工具包，包含三个组件：1）将人口统计平等、均等机会和平等机会差异扩展到交叉群体的观察框架；2）评估基于治疗背景下公平性的反事实框架；3）评估对交叉群体成员资格干预下公平性的广义反事实框架。使用All of Us Controlled Tier V8数据集的电子健康记录数据，在青光眼手术预测任务中评估了该工具包。

Result: 观察性分析发现尽管模型性能适中（AUROC = 0.709；准确率 = 0.651），但仍存在显著的交叉差异。交叉评估揭示了比单一轴分析更大的公平性差距，包括人口统计平等差异为0.20，均等机会的真阳性率和假阳性率差距分别为0.33和0.15。基于排列的零分布反事实分析产生的"u值"估计接近零，表明在以协变量为条件后，观察到的差异与偶然性一致。

Conclusion: Fairlogue提供了一个模块化工具包，整合了观察性和反事实方法，用于量化和评估临床机器学习工作流程中的交叉偏倚。

Abstract: Objective: Algorithmic fairness is essential for equitable and trustworthy machine learning in healthcare. Most fairness tools emphasize single-axis demographic comparisons and may miss compounded disparities affecting intersectional populations. This study introduces Fairlogue, a toolkit designed to operationalize intersectional fairness assessment in observational and counterfactual contexts within clinical settings. Methods: Fairlogue is a Python-based toolkit composed of three components: 1) an observational framework extending demographic parity, equalized odds, and equal opportunity difference to intersectional populations; 2) a counterfactual framework evaluating fairness under treatment-based contexts; and 3) a generalized counterfactual framework assessing fairness under interventions on intersectional group membership. The toolkit was evaluated using electronic health record data from the All of Us Controlled Tier V8 dataset in a glaucoma surgery prediction task using logistic regression with race and gender as protected attributes. Results: Observational analysis identified substantial intersectional disparities despite moderate model performance (AUROC = 0.709; accuracy = 0.651). Intersectional evaluation revealed larger fairness gaps than single-axis analyses, including demographic parity differences of 0.20 and equalized odds true positive and false positive rate gaps of 0.33 and 0.15, respectively. Counterfactual analysis using permutation-based null distributions produced unfairness ("u-value") estimates near zero, suggesting observed disparities were consistent with chance after conditioning on covariates. Conclusion: Fairlogue provides a modular toolkit integrating observational and counterfactual methods for quantifying and evaluating intersectional bias in clinical machine learning workflows.

</details>


### [250] [Noise Immunity in In-Context Tabular Learning: An Empirical Robustness Analysis of TabPFN's Attention Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04868)
*James Hu,Mahdi Ghelichi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大量实验证明，表格基础模型TabPFN在面对无关特征、特征相关性和标签噪声等数据质量问题时，能够保持极高的鲁棒性、预测性能和内部注意力机制的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融和医疗等工业领域，表格数据预测非常普遍，但针对每个新表格重新训练专属模型成本高昂甚至不可行，且数据常存在无关预测变量、特征组相关性和标签噪声等质量问题。因此，研究表格基础模型在这些次优条件下的鲁棒性具有重要的现实意义。

Method: 针对二分类问题，使用受控合成扰动进行参数化测试，变化维度包括：(i) 通过注入随机不相关特征和引入非线性相关特征来改变数据集宽度；(ii) 通过增加训练行数来改变数据集大小；(iii) 通过增加错误标记目标的比例来改变标签质量。除预测性能外，还分析了注意力集中度和基于注意力的特征排名指标等内部信号，并结合注意力热力图、特征-词元嵌入和SHAP图进行定性可视化分析。

Result: 在所有参数化测试中，TabPFN表现出非凡的弹性：ROC-AUC保持高位，注意力结构保持清晰锐利，信息性特征在基于注意力的指标中排名靠前。可视化分析显示，各层呈现出一致模式，TabPFN逐渐集中关注有用特征并将其信号与噪声分离。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，TabPFN是一种鲁棒的表格基础模型，能够在各种数据不完善场景下同时保持预测性能和内部行为的连贯性。这为其在工业领域的实际应用提供了强有力的实证支持。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) such as TabPFN (Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network) are designed to generalize across heterogeneous tabular datasets through in-context learning (ICL). They perform prediction in a single forward pass conditioned on labeled examples without dataset-specific parameter updates. This paradigm is particularly attractive in industrial domains (e.g., finance and healthcare) where tabular prediction is pervasive. Retraining a bespoke model for each new table can be costly or infeasible in these settings, while data quality issues such as irrelevant predictors, correlated feature groups, and label noise are common. In this paper, we provide strong empirical evidence that TabPFN is highly robust under these sub-optimal conditions. We study TabPFN and its attention mechanisms for binary classification problems with controlled synthetic perturbations that vary: (i) dataset width by injecting random uncorrelated features and by introducing nonlinearly correlated features, (ii) dataset size by increasing the number of training rows, and (iii) label quality by increasing the fraction of mislabeled targets. Beyond predictive performance, we analyze internal signals including attention concentration and attention-based feature ranking metrics. Across these parametric tests, TabPFN is remarkably resilient: ROC-AUC remains high, attention stays structured and sharp, and informative features are highly ranked by attention-based metrics. Qualitative visualizations with attention heatmaps, feature-token embeddings, and SHAP plots further support a consistent pattern across layers in which TabPFN increasingly concentrates on useful features while separating their signals from noise. Together, these findings suggest that TabPFN is a robust TFM capable of maintaining both predictive performance and coherent internal behavior under various scenarios of data imperfections.

</details>


### [251] [Optimizing LLM Prompt Engineering with DSPy Based Declarative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04869)
*Shiek Ruksana,Sailesh Kiran Kurra,Thipparthi Sanjay Baradwaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于DSPy框架的声明式提示优化方法，通过自动化、模块化的提示构建，在推理任务和检索增强生成等基准测试中，将事实准确性提高了30-45%，并将幻觉率降低了约25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）的性能高度依赖于提示设计，而传统的提示工程方法主要依赖启发式的试错过程，这在可扩展性、可复现性和跨任务泛化能力方面存在局限性。因此，研究旨在探索一种更系统化、自动化的提示优化框架。

Method: 引入了一种统一的DSPy LLM架构，该架构结合了符号规划、无梯度优化和自动化模块重写技术。通过声明式学习方法，实现了提示的自动合成、校正、校准以及自适应推理控制，从而减少幻觉、增强事实基础并避免不必要的提示复杂性。

Result: 在推理任务、检索增强生成和多步思维链基准测试上的实验评估表明，该方法在输出可靠性、效率和跨模型泛化能力方面均有一致的提升。具体而言，事实准确性提升了30-45%，幻觉率降低了约25%。

Conclusion: 基于DSPy的声明式提示优化框架能够有效替代传统的启发式方法，显著提升LLM系统的性能与可靠性。论文最后指出了当前方法的局限性，并讨论了声明式提示优化框架的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong performance across a wide range of natural language processing tasks; however, their effectiveness is highly dependent on prompt design, structure, and embedded reasoning signals. Conventional prompt engineering methods largely rely on heuristic trial-and-error processes, which limits scalability, reproducibility, and generalization across tasks. DSPy, a declarative framework for optimizing text-processing pipelines, offers an alternative approach by enabling automated, modular, and learnable prompt construction for LLM-based systems.This paper presents a systematic study of DSPy-based declarative learning for prompt optimization, with emphasis on prompt synthesis, correction, calibration, and adaptive reasoning control. We introduce a unified DSPy LLM architecture that combines symbolic planning, gradient free optimization, and automated module rewriting to reduce hallucinations, improve factual grounding, and avoid unnecessary prompt complexity. Experimental evaluations conducted on reasoning tasks, retrieval-augmented generation, and multi-step chain-of-thought benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains in output reliability, efficiency, and generalization across models. The results show improvements of up to 30 to 45% in factual accuracy and a reduction of approximately 25% in hallucination rates. Finally, we outline key limitations and discuss future research directions for declarative prompt optimization frameworks.

</details>


### [252] [Data Attribution in Adaptive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04892)
*Amit Kiran Rege*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对自适应学习场景中现有归因方法的局限性，提出了新的数据归因方法框架，并在特定结构类别中实现了可识别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在在线赌博机、强化学习和大语言模型后训练等自适应学习场景中，训练观测数据不仅更新模型，还会改变未来数据的分布。现有面向静态数据集的归因方法忽略了这种反馈机制，无法准确衡量单个观测数据对最终学习结果的影响，因此需要专门针对自适应学习场景设计归因方法。

Method: 作者通过条件干预目标形式化了有限视界自适应学习中的实例级归因问题，证明了仅使用回放端信息无法恢复目标，并在特定结构类别中找到了可以从日志数据中识别目标的解决方案。研究采用因果推断的理论框架，分析了数据收集过程中的反馈机制。

Result: 论文证明了在一般情况下，回放端信息无法恢复自适应学习中的条件干预目标；同时识别出了一个特定的结构类别，在该类别中目标可以从日志数据中被识别出来，从而实现了数据归因的可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究为自适应学习场景中的数据归因问题建立了理论基础，明确了归因可行性的边界条件，为后续开发实用的自适应学习数据归因算法提供了理论指导和方法框架。

Abstract: Machine learning models increasingly generate their own training data -- online bandits, reinforcement learning, and post-training pipelines for language models are leading examples. In these adaptive settings, a single training observation both updates the learner and shifts the distribution of future data the learner will collect. Standard attribution methods, designed for static datasets, ignore this feedback. We formalize occurrence-level attribution for finite-horizon adaptive learning via a conditional interventional target, prove that replay-side information cannot recover it in general, and identify a structural class in which the target is identified from logged data.

</details>


### [253] [Are Latent Reasoning Models Easily Interpretable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04902)
*Connor Dilgren,Sarah Wiegreffe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过调查两个最先进的潜在推理模型，发现潜在推理token常非必要，但在必要时可解码出可解释的推理轨迹，表明当前LRMs主要编码可解释过程且可解释性可作为预测正确性的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在推理模型（LRMs）因推理成本低且理论上能并行探索多条推理路径而备受关注，但其不使用自然语言推理导致可解释性降低，难以监控。因此，本文旨在调查LRMs的可解释性。

Method: 研究两个最先进的LRMs；分析潜在推理token的必要性；在必要时解码自然语言推理轨迹；提出一种无需先验黄金推理轨迹即可从潜在token解码验证过的自然语言推理轨迹的方法。

Result: 在逻辑推理数据集上，LRMs几乎总能不使用潜在推理产生相同答案；当潜在推理token必要时，可在65-93%的正确预测实例中解码出黄金推理轨迹；提出的方法能对多数正确预测解码出验证轨迹，但仅对少数错误预测有效。

Conclusion: 当前LRMs主要编码可解释的过程，可解释性本身可作为预测正确性的信号。

Abstract: Latent reasoning models (LRMs) have attracted significant research interest due to their low inference cost (relative to explicit reasoning models) and theoretical ability to explore multiple reasoning paths in parallel. However, these benefits come at the cost of reduced interpretability: LRMs are difficult to monitor because they do not reason in natural language. This paper presents an investigation into LRM interpretability by examining two state-of-the-art LRMs. First, we find that latent reasoning tokens are often unnecessary for LRMs' predictions; on logical reasoning datasets, LRMs can almost always produce the same final answers without using latent reasoning at all. This underutilization of reasoning tokens may partially explain why LRMs do not consistently outperform explicit reasoning methods and raises doubts about the stated role of these tokens in prior work. Second, we demonstrate that when latent reasoning tokens are necessary for performance, we can decode gold reasoning traces up to 65-93% of the time for correctly predicted instances. This suggests LRMs often implement the expected solution rather than an uninterpretable reasoning process. Finally, we present a method to decode a verified natural language reasoning trace from latent tokens without knowing a gold reasoning trace a priori, demonstrating that it is possible to find a verified trace for a majority of correct predictions but only a minority of incorrect predictions. Our findings highlight that current LRMs largely encode interpretable processes, and interpretability itself can be a signal of prediction correctness.

</details>


### [254] [HI-MoE: Hierarchical Instance-Conditioned Mixture-of-Experts for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04908)
*Vadim Vashkelis,Natalia Trukhina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HI-MoE，一种用于目标检测的层次化实例条件混合专家架构，通过场景和实例两级路由机制提升性能，在小物体检测上表现尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉混合专家（MoE）方法主要在图像或patch层面进行路由，这种粒度与目标检测任务不匹配，因为检测的基本推理单元是对象查询（object query）。需要一种能够适应目标检测异构、以实例为中心结构的稀疏计算方法。

Method: 提出层次化实例条件混合专家（HI-MoE）架构，包含两级路由机制：1）轻量级场景路由器首先选择场景一致的专家子集；2）实例路由器将每个对象查询分配给该子集中的少量专家。这种设计在保持稀疏计算的同时更好地匹配检测任务的实例中心结构。

Result: 在COCO数据集上的实验表明，HI-MoE相比密集DINO基线以及更简单的token级或仅实例路由变体均有提升，特别是在小物体检测上取得了显著增益。在LVIS数据集上进行了初步的专业化分析。提供了专家专业化模式的可视化。

Conclusion: HI-MoE成功地将MoE架构适配到目标检测任务，通过层次化路由机制实现了与检测任务特性更好的对齐，为后续实验验证提供了方法论基础和初步实证支持。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable conditional computation by activating only a subset of model parameters for each input. Although sparse routing has been highly effective in language models and has also shown promise in vision, most vision MoE methods operate at the image or patch level. This granularity is poorly aligned with object detection, where the fundamental unit of reasoning is an object query corresponding to a candidate instance. We propose Hierarchical Instance-Conditioned Mixture-of-Experts (HI-MoE), a DETR-style detection architecture that performs routing in two stages: a lightweight scene router first selects a scene-consistent expert subset, and an instance router then assigns each object query to a small number of experts within that subset. This design aims to preserve sparse computation while better matching the heterogeneous, instance-centric structure of detection. In the current draft, experiments are concentrated on COCO with preliminary specialization analysis on LVIS. Under these settings, HI-MoE improves over a dense DINO baseline and over simpler token-level or instance-only routing variants, with especially strong gains on small objects. We also provide an initial visualization of expert specialization patterns. We present the method, ablations, and current limitations in a form intended to support further experimental validation.

</details>


### [255] [Empowering Power Outage Prediction with Spatially Aware Hybrid Graph Neural Networks and Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04916)
*Xuyang Shen,Zijie Pan,Diego Cerrai,Xinxuan Zhang,Christopher Colorio,Emmanouil N. Anagnostou,Dongjin Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于空间感知的混合图神经网络（SA-HGNN）方法，结合对比学习技术，用于预测极端天气引发的电力中断，显著提升了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端天气事件导致的电力中断对经济和社会造成严重影响，现有的停电预测模型（OPM）未能充分考虑极端天气事件的空间效应，限制了预测精度。

Method: 提出空间感知混合图神经网络（SA-HGNN），首先编码静态特征（如土地覆盖、基础设施）和事件特定动态特征（如风速、降水）的空间关系；其次利用对比学习解决不同极端天气事件类型的不平衡问题，通过最小化相似位置的事件内距离并最大化所有位置的事件间距离来生成位置特定的嵌入表示。

Result: 在康涅狄格州、马萨诸塞州西部、马萨诸塞州东部和新罕布什尔州四个公用事业服务区域的实证研究表明，SA-HGNN在电力中断预测方面达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SA-HGNN通过融合空间关系和对比学习，有效解决了极端天气事件电力中断预测中的空间效应和类别不平衡问题，为电力系统提供了更准确的预测工具。

Abstract: Extreme weather events, such as severe storms, hurricanes, snowstorms, and ice storms, which are exacerbated by climate change, frequently cause widespread power outages. These outages halt industrial operations, impact communities, damage critical infrastructure, profoundly disrupt economies, and have far-reaching effects across various sectors. To mitigate these effects, the University of Connecticut and Eversource Energy Center have developed an outage prediction modeling (OPM) system to provide pre-emptive forecasts for electric distribution networks before such weather events occur. However, existing predictive models in the system do not incorporate the spatial effect of extreme weather events. To this end, we develop Spatially Aware Hybrid Graph Neural Networks (SA-HGNN) with contrastive learning to enhance the OPM predictions for extreme weather-induced power outages. Specifically, we first encode spatial relationships of both static features (e.g., land cover, infrastructure) and event-specific dynamic features (e.g., wind speed, precipitation) via Spatially Aware Hybrid Graph Neural Networks (SA-HGNN). Next, we leverage contrastive learning to handle the imbalance problem associated with different types of extreme weather events and generate location-specific embeddings by minimizing intra-event distances between similar locations while maximizing inter-event distances across all locations. Thorough empirical studies in four utility service territories, i.e., Connecticut, Western Massachusetts, Eastern Massachusetts, and New Hampshire, demonstrate that SA-HGNN can achieve state-of-the-art performance for power outage prediction.

</details>


### [256] [Stratifying Reinforcement Learning with Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04923)
*Justin Curry,Alberto Speranzon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了分层理论与信号时序逻辑（STL）之间的对应原理，将STL原子谓词解释为分层空间中的成员测试，为分析深度强化学习嵌入空间结构提供了新的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立分层理论与信号时序逻辑之间的理论联系，为深度强化学习生成的嵌入空间提供几何解释，并为高维分析提供原则性框架。

Method: 开发基于分层的STL语义，将原子谓词解释为分层空间的成员测试；在Minigrid游戏中验证理论，并对使用STL鲁棒性作为奖励的DRL智能体潜在嵌入应用数值技术，提出计算高效的签名方法。

Result: 揭示了STL公式可视为诱导时空分层；提出了能高效揭示嵌入空间分层结构的计算签名方法；初步证据表明该方法在Minigrid游戏中具有应用潜力。

Conclusion: 分层理论为STL提供了新的语义解释，建立了与深度强化学习嵌入空间的联系，既支持现有高维分析工具的重用，又激发了新型计算技术的开发。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a stratification-based semantics for Signal Temporal Logic (STL) in which each atomic predicate is interpreted as a membership test in a stratified space. This perspective reveals a novel correspondence principle between stratification theory and STL, showing that most STL formulas can be viewed as inducing a stratification of space-time. The significance of this interpretation is twofold. First, it offers a fresh theoretical framework for analyzing the structure of the embedding space generated by deep reinforcement learning (DRL) and relates it to the geometry of the ambient decision space. Second, it provides a principled framework that both enables the reuse of existing high-dimensional analysis tools and motivates the creation of novel computational techniques. To ground the theory, we (1) illustrate the role of stratification theory in Minigrid games and (2) apply numerical techniques to the latent embeddings of a DRL agent playing such a game where the robustness of STL formulas is used as the reward. In the process, we propose computationally efficient signatures that, based on preliminary evidence, appear promising for uncovering the stratification structure of such embedding spaces.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [Minos: Systematically Classifying Performance and Power Characteristics of GPU Workloads on HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03591)
*Rutwik Jain,Yiwei Jiang,Matthew D. Sinclair,Shivaraman Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Minos，一种通过低成本分析识别应用特征的系统化分类机制，可将工作负载分组为有限类别，在预测功率和性能时分别实现4%和3%的平均误差，并将新应用的性能分析时间减少89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模HPC集群日益受到功率约束，而现代应用程序常出现功率峰值。由于应用日益多样化且需要针对性优化，传统通过性能分析进行优化的方法面临规模大、算法多样性和缺乏有效工具等挑战。

Method: 提出Minos系统化分类机制，通过低成本性能分析识别相似的应用特征，将行为相似的工作负载分组为有限数量的不同类别，从而减少对新工作负载进行广泛性能分析的开销。

Result: 在18个流行的图分析、HPC、HPC+ML和ML工作负载上，Minos在功率预测方面达到4%的平均误差，在性能预测方面达到3%的平均误差，相比最先进方法提高了10%的预测精度；在预测未见应用的频率限制行为时，减少了89%的性能分析时间。

Conclusion: Minos能够有效解决HPC系统中功率和性能联合优化的挑战，通过系统化的应用分类显著降低性能分析开销，同时保持高预测精度，为当前和未来HPC系统的功率性能优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: As large-scale HPC compute clusters increasingly adopt accelerators such as GPUs to meet the voracious demands of modern workloads, these clusters are increasingly becoming power constrained. Unfortunately, modern applications can often temporarily exceed the power ratings of the accelerators ("power spikes"). Thus, current and future HPC systems must optimize for both power and performance together. However, this is made difficult by increasingly diverse applications, which often require bespoke optimizations to run efficiently on each cluster. Traditionally researchers overcome this problem by profiling applications on specific clusters and optimizing, but the scale, algorithmic diversity, and lack of effective tools make this challenging. To overcome these inefficiencies, we propose Minos, a systematic classification mechanism that identifies similar application characteristics via low-cost profiling for power and performance. This allows us to group similarly behaving workloads into a finite number of distinct classes and reduce the overhead of extensively profiling new workloads. For example, when predicting frequency capping behavior for a previously unseen application, Minos reduces profiling time by 89%. Moreover, across 18 popular graph analytics, HPC, HPC+ML, and ML workloads, Minos achieves a mean error of 4% for power predictions and 3% for performance predictions, significantly improving predictions over state-of-the-art approaches by 10%.

</details>


### [258] [DéjàVu: A Minimalistic Mechanism for Distributed Plurality Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03648)
*Francesco d'Amore,Niccolò D'Archivio,George Giakkoupis,Frédéric Giroire,Emanuele Natale*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DéjàVu的全新极简协议，用于解决分布式系统中的多数派共识问题。该协议仅需智能体检测重复意见，无需维护计数器或设置参数，在特定场景下比经典的h-majority协议更具通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式系统的多数派共识问题中，经典的h-majority协议虽然简单且被广泛研究，但它需要智能体维护计数器、估计频率或预设采样参数h。本文旨在寻找一种更简单、无需参数调优且资源消耗更低的机制，以解决大规模简单智能体群体的共识问题。

Method: 提出DéjàVu机制：每个智能体随机查询邻居的意见，直到遇到某个意见第二次出现（即检测到重复），随后将自身意见更新为该重复值。该机制仅依赖检测重复这一基本能力，不需要计数器、频率估计或参数设置。文章结合多个具有独立价值的技术手段对协议进行了严格的理论分析。

Result: 理论分析表明，DéjàVu协议在性能上可与h-majority协议相媲美，并且在某些参数区间内，其通信效率显著优于h-majority。这证明了其在多数派共识问题上的有效性和竞争力。

Conclusion: DéjàVu提供了一种极其简单且强大的多数派共识原语。它证明了仅依赖“检测重复”这一基本能力即可实现高效共识，避免了复杂的内部状态维护和参数调优，为分布式系统设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We study the plurality consensus problem in distributed systems where a population of extremely simple agents, each initially holding one of k opinions, aims to agree on the initially most frequent one.
  In this setting, h-majority is arguably the simplest and most studied protocol, in which each agent samples the opinion of h neighbors uniformly at random and updates its opinion to the most frequent value in the sample.
  We propose a new, extremely simple mechanism called DéjàVu: an agent queries neighbors until it encounters an opinion for the second time, at which point it updates its own opinion to the duplicate value. This rule does not require agents to maintain counters or estimate frequencies, nor to choose any parameter (such as a sample size h); it relies solely on the primitive ability to detect repetition.
  We provide a rigorous analysis of DéjàVu that relies on several technical ideas of independent interest and demonstrates that it is competitive with h-majority and, in some regimes, substantially more communication-efficient, thus yielding a powerful primitive for plurality consensus.

</details>


### [259] [Deploy, Calibrate, Monitor, Heal -- No Human Required: An Autonomous AI SRE Agent for Elasticsearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03933)
*Muhamed Ramees Cheriya Mukkolakkal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ES Guardian Agent，一个基于LLM的自治AI SRE系统，能够无人工干预管理Elasticsearch集群全生命周期，并通过多源预测引擎实现故障预测，实现了99.9999%的可用性目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模运行Elasticsearch集群需要持续的人工专业知识，涵盖从初始部署到性能调优、监控、故障预测和事件恢复的全生命周期，这带来了巨大的运维成本和挑战。作者希望构建一个自主AI系统来完全接管这些复杂的运维工作，消除对人工干预的依赖。

Method: 系统通过11个阶段管理Elasticsearch完整生命周期：Evaluate、Optimize、Deploy、Calibrate、Stabilize、Alert、Predict、Heal、Learn和Upgrade。核心技术是多云源预测故障引擎，持续摄取并关联指标趋势、应用日志和内核级遥测数据（包括Linux dmesg流、NVMe SMART数据、NIC绑定统计和热传感器），提前数小时预测故障。系统还通过将当前系统签名与已解决故障的持久事件记忆交叉引用，主动规划纠正措施。最终版本包含4,589行代码，具有五个监控层和一个迭代AI行动循环。

Result: 在生产评估中，Guardian Agent执行了300次自主调查与修复循环，使集群从18小时的跨系统故障中恢复，诊断出所有主机节点的硬件NIC故障，并保持持续的运营可见性。此外，研究确定每个分片的数据量（而非调优参数）是查询性能的主要决定因素，延迟随每MB/分片按0.26ms的比例增长。系统成功实现了99.9999%可用性目标。

Conclusion: 证明了配备工具使用能力的LLM可以作为全生命周期自主SRE发挥作用，能够管理复杂的Elasticsearch集群运维。通过多源预测引擎和持久事件记忆机制，系统实现了提前数小时预测故障并主动采取纠正措施的能力。同时发现了分片数据量与查询延迟之间的线性关系，为Elasticsearch性能优化提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Operating Elasticsearch clusters at scale demands continuous human expertise spanning the full lifecycle -- from initial deployment through performance tuning, monitoring, failure prediction, and incident recovery. We present the ES Guardian Agent, an autonomous AI SRE system that manages the complete Elasticsearch lifecycle without human intervention through eleven distinct phases: Evaluate, Optimize, Deploy, Calibrate, Stabilize, Alert, Predict, Heal, Learn, and Upgrade. A critical differentiator is its multi-source predictive failure engine, which continuously ingests and correlates metrics trends, application logs, and kernel-level telemetry -- including Linux dmesg streams, NVMe SMART data, NIC bond statistics, and thermal sensors -- to anticipate failures hours before they materialize. By cross-referencing current system signatures against a persistent incident memory of resolved failures, the AI engine stages corrective actions proactively. Through four successive agent architectures -- culminating in a 4,589-line system with five monitoring layers and an iterative AI action loop -- we demonstrate that an LLM equipped with tool-use access can function as a full-lifecycle autonomous SRE targeting six-nines (99.9999%) availability. In production evaluation, the Guardian Agent executed 300 autonomous investigation-and-repair cycles, recovered a cluster from an 18-hour cross-system outage, diagnosed hardware NIC failures across all host nodes, and maintained continuous operational visibility. We establish that data volume per shard -- not tuning -- is the primary determinant of query performance, with latency scaling at 0.26 ms per MB/shard.

</details>


### [260] [Lemonshark: Asynchronous DAG-BFT With Early Finality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03974)
*Michael Yiqing Hu,Alvin Hong Yao Yan,Yang Yihan,Liu Xiang,Li Jialin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Lemonshark，一种异步DAG-BFT协议，通过在交易层级重新解释DAG并识别提交的充分条件，使节点能在官方提交前安全地敲定交易，从而将延迟降低了高达65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DAG-BFT协议（如DAG-Rider）虽实现了高吞吐量，但仅对领导者区块提供最优延迟；非领导者区块无法独立提交，导致整体延迟性能受限。

Method: 在交易层级而非区块层级重新解释DAG结构，识别出提交对于安全结果而言是充分但不必要的条件，允许节点在官方提交之前安全地敲定交易。

Result: 与最先进的异步BFT协议相比，Lemonshark实现了高达65%的延迟降低。

Conclusion: 通过重新审视DAG结构并利用交易级优化，Lemonshark在不牺牲正确性的前提下，成功解决了非领导者区块延迟高的问题，显著提升了异步DAG-BFT协议的性能。

Abstract: DAG-Rider popularized a new paradigm of DAG-BFT protocols, separating dissemination from consensus: all nodes disseminate transactions as blocks that reference previously known blocks, while consensus is reached by electing certain blocks as leaders. This design yields high throughput but confers optimal latency only to leader blocks; non-leader blocks cannot be committed independently.
  We present Lemonshark, an asynchronous DAG-BFT protocol that reinterprets the DAG at a transactional level and identifies conditions where commitment is sufficient -- but not necessary -- for safe results, enabling nodes to finalize transactions before official commitment, without compromising correctness. Compared to the state-of-the-art asynchronous BFT protocol, Lemonshark reduces latency by up to 65\%.

</details>


### [261] [Ledger-State Stigmergy: A Formal Framework for Indirect Coordination Grounded in Distributed Ledger State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03997)
*Fernando Paredes García*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将区块链上自主代理通过共享账本状态进行的间接协调机制形式化为“基于账本状态的间接协调”，识别了三种基础链上协调模式，为去中心化应用层协调提供了可复用的词汇表和设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链上的清算机器人、套利机器人等自主代理通过读取共享账本状态（余额、合约存储、事件日志）而非直接消息交换来协调行动，这种机制与Grassé于1959年提出的“共识主动性”（stigmergy）高度相似。尽管共识主动性在群体智能和多智能体系统中有成熟的形式化描述，且链上代理在实践中已表现出共识主动性行为，但此前缺乏一个应用层框架将两者清晰地连接起来。

Method: 提出了“基于账本状态的间接协调”这一账本特定的应用层定义，将Grassé的共识主动性机制映射到分布式账本技术；采用状态转换形式化方法；识别了三种反复出现的基础链上协调模式（状态标志State-Flag、事件信号Event-Signal、阈值触发Threshold-Trigger）以及提交-揭示序列覆盖层；通过状态标志任务板示例，将账本状态协调与链下消息传递和集中式编排进行分析比较。

Result: 建立了基于账本状态的间接协调的形式化映射；识别并形式化了三种基础链上协调模式和一个序列覆盖层；通过案例比较分析，展示了账本状态协调相对于传统方法的特点；贡献了一套可复用的词汇表、账本特定的形式映射，以及在复制共享状态上进行去中心化协调的设计指导。

Conclusion: 基于账本状态的间接协调为去中心化应用层的协调问题提供了坚实的理论基础和实用框架，使开发者和研究人员能够更清晰地理解、设计和分析区块链上自主代理的协调机制。

Abstract: Autonomous software agents on blockchains solve distributed-coordination problems by reading shared ledger state instead of exchanging direct messages. Liquidation keepers, arbitrage bots, and other autonomous on-chain agents watch balances, contract storage, and event logs; when conditions change, they act. The ledger therefore functions as a replicated shared-state medium through which decentralized agents coordinate indirectly. This form of indirect coordination mirrors what Grassé called stigmergy in 1959: organisms coordinating through traces left in a shared environment, with no central plan.
  Stigmergy has mature formalizations in swarm intelligence and multi-agent systems, and on-chain agents already behave stigmergically in practice, but no prior application-layer framework cleanly bridges the two. We introduce Indirect coordination grounded in ledger state (Coordinación indirecta basada en el estado del registro contable) as a ledger-specific applied definition that maps Grassé's mechanism onto distributed ledger technology. We operationalize this with a state-transition formalism, identify three recurring base on-chain coordination patterns (State-Flag, Event-Signal, Threshold- Trigger) together with a Commit-Reveal sequencing overlay, and work through a State-Flag task-board example to compare ledger-state coordination analytically with off-chain messaging and centralized orchestration. The contribution is a reusable vocabulary, a ledger-specific formal mapping, and design guidance for decentralized coordination over replicated shared state at the application layer.

</details>


### [262] [Round-Delayed Amnesiac Flooding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04260)
*Oluwatobi Alafin,George B. Mertzios,Paul G. Spirakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文全面分析了引入回合异步的RDAF协议，证明了其在无环图上必然终止，在有环图上可能不终止，并揭示了终止问题在一般情况下的不可判定性及在特定受限 adversary 下的可判定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解在存在对抗性延迟（通信延迟）的环境下，泛洪（Flooding）算法的行为及其终止特性，从而为设计健壮的分布式协议提供理论基础。

Method: 研究者提出了RDAF的形式化模型以引入基于回合的异步性。他们利用图论方法区分无环图与有环图进行证明，并引入“最终周期对手”（Eventually Periodic Adversaries, EPA）这一受限模型来分析终止问题的可判定性。

Result: 主要结果包括：1. 证明了在存在对抗性延迟的情况下，泛洪算法在无环图上总是终止；2. 构造了反例表明在任何有环图上都存在不终止的可能性；3. 证明了在任意可计算对手面前，终止问题是不可判定的；4. 证明了在EPA模型下，终止问题变得可判定。

Conclusion: 该研究深化了对通信延迟环境下泛洪算法行为的理解，界定了算法终止的边界条件，并为未来设计能够应对延迟的鲁棒分布式协议提供了关键见解。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive analysis of Round-Delayed Amnesiac Flooding (RDAF), a variant of Amnesiac Flooding that introduces round-based asynchrony through adversarial delays. We establish fundamental properties of RDAF, including termination characteristics for different graph types and decidability results under various adversarial models. Our key contributions include: (1) a formal model of RDAF incorporating round-based asynchrony, (2) a proof that flooding always terminates on acyclic graphs despite adversarial delays, (3) a construction showing non-termination is possible on any cyclic graph, (4) a demonstration that termination is undecidable with arbitrary computable adversaries, and (5) the introduction of Eventually Periodic Adversaries (EPA) under which termination becomes decidable. These results enhance our understanding of flooding in communication-delay settings and provide insights for designing robust distributed protocols.

</details>


### [263] [GENSERVE: Efficient Co-Serving of Heterogeneous Diffusion Model Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04335)
*Fanjiang Ye,Zhangke Li,Xinrui Zhong,Ethan Ma,Russell Chen,Kaijian Wang,Jingwei Zuo,Desen Sun,Ye Cao,Triston Cao,Myungjin Lee,Arvind Krishnamurthy,Yuke Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了GENSERVE系统，通过利用扩散模型推理过程的可预测性和可抢占性，实现文本到图像(T2I)和文本到视频(T2V)工作负载的高效协同服务，显著提升了服务等级目标(SLO)达成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的服务系统在共享GPU集群上同时服务T2I和T2V请求时面临挑战，因为这两种工作负载具有截然不同的计算需求、并行特性和延迟要求，导致严重的SLO违规问题。

Method: 利用扩散推理在离散、可预测步骤中进行且在步骤边界天然可抢占的特性，引入步骤级资源自适应机制：智能视频抢占、具有动态批处理的弹性序列并行性，以及SLO感知调度器。

Result: 实验结果表明，在各种配置下，GENSERVE相比最强基线将SLO达成率提升了高达44%。

Conclusion: 扩散过程的内在可预测性为异构感知资源管理开辟了新的设计空间，基于步骤级资源自适应的协同服务系统能够有效解决T2I和T2V混合工作负载的服务效率问题。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as the prevailing approach for text-to-image (T2I) and text-to-video (T2V) generation, yet production platforms must increasingly serve both modalities on shared GPU clusters while meeting stringent latency SLOs. Co-serving such heterogeneous workloads is challenging: T2I and T2V requests exhibit vastly different compute demands, parallelism characteristics, and latency requirements, leading to significant SLO violations in existing serving systems. We present GENSERVE, a co-serving system that leverages the inherent predictability of the diffusion process to optimize serving efficiency. A central insight is that diffusion inference proceeds in discrete, predictable steps and is naturally preemptible at step boundaries, opening a new design space for heterogeneity-aware resource management. GENSERVE introduces step-level resource adaptation through three coordinated mechanisms: intelligent video preemption, elastic sequence parallelism with dynamic batching, and an SLO-aware scheduler that jointly optimizes resource allocation across all concurrent requests. Experimental results show that GENSERVE improves the SLO attainment rate by up to 44% over the strongest baseline across diverse configurations.

</details>


### [264] [An experimental evaluation of satellite constellation emulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04498)
*Victor Cionca,Ferenc Szabo,Stanimir Vasilev,Dylan Smyth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对StarryNet、OpenSN和Celestial三个开源卫星仿真软件进行了深入评估，通过与真实世界测量数据的对比，识别了现有仿真技术的不足并提出了未来的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏对物理测试平台的访问，卫星仿真软件在研究中至关重要。然而，现有的仿真软件必须生成与真实世界观测数据高度一致的结果，并且在设置和运行实验时具有可接受的资源开销，因此需要评估现有仿真软件的性能。

Method: 本研究对三个开源仿真软件（StarryNet、OpenSN和Celestial）进行了并行运行测试，并将其生成的观测数据与WetLinks研究的真实世界测量数据进行对比分析。

Result: 通过对仿真结果与真实测量数据的比较，识别了当前卫星仿真技术的不足之处，同时也发现了具有潜力的研究和开发方向。

Conclusion: 当前卫星仿真技术在生成与真实世界一致的观测数据方面存在局限性，未来研究应致力于改进仿真技术的准确性和资源效率，以更好地支持卫星网络研究。

Abstract: Satellite emulation software is essential for research due to the lack of access to physical testbeds. To be useful, emulators must generate observations that are well-aligned with real-world ones, and they must have acceptable resource overheads for setting up and running experiments. This study provides an in-depth evaluation of three open-source emulators: StarryNet, OpenSN, and Celestial. Running them side-by-side and comparing them with real-world measurements from the WetLinks study identifies shortcomings of current satellite emulation techniques as well as promising avenues for research and development.

</details>


### [265] [NBI-Slurm: Simplified submission of Slurm jobs with energy saving mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04558)
*Andrea Telatin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: NBI-Slurm是一个Perl包，为SLURM HPC集群提供简化的作业提交和管理接口，具有TUI应用、工具包装器生成和“生态模式”节能调度功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLURM高性能计算集群的原生接口认知负担较高，用户难以高效管理作业。同时，研究机构需要降低计算碳足迹，但手动规划作业提交时间对用户而言负担较重。

Method: 开发了NBI-Slurm Perl包，提供两类解决方案：（a）用于程序化作业管理的Perl模块库；（b）降低认知负担的命令行工具套件。特色功能包括：TUI应用程序用于查看和取消作业、为生物信息学工具生成特定包装器、以及“生态模式”能源感知调度。

Result: 成功实现了简化的SLURM接口，包括TUI应用、工具包装器生成功能和创新的“生态模式”调度功能，该模式能自动将灵活作业推迟到非高峰期执行。

Conclusion: NBI-Slurm有效降低了SLURM接口的认知负担，同时通过自动化节能调度帮助研究机构减少计算碳足迹，无需用户手动规划提交时间。

Abstract: NBI-Slurm is a Perl package that provides a simplified, user-friendly interface for submitting and managing jobs on SLURM high-performance computing (HPC) clusters. It offers both a library of Perl modules for programmatic job management and a suite of command-line tools designed to reduce the cognitive overhead of SLURM's native interface. Distinctive features of NBI-Slurm are (a) TUI applications to view and cancel jobs, (b) the possibility to generate tool-specific wrappers for (bioinformatic) tools and (c) an energy-aware scheduling mode -- "eco mode" -- that automatically defers flexible jobs to off-peak periods, helping research institutions reduce their computational carbon footprint without requiring users to manually plan submission times.

</details>


### [266] [LP-GEMM: Integrating Layout Propagation into GEMM Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04599)
*César Guedes Carneiro,Lucas Alvarenga,Guido Araujo,Sandro Rigo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出LP-GEMM方法，通过在连续GEMM操作间传播数据布局来消除冗余的打包/解包操作，在x86和RISC-V架构上相比OpenBLAS平均加速2.25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学计算和机器学习工作负载中，连续的依赖性GEMM操作通常占主导执行时间。现有BLAS库虽然对单个GEMM调用进行了优化，但受限于BLAS API约束，每个调用都需要独立打包输入矩阵并将输出恢复到规范内存布局。在连续GEMM场景下，这些约束导致冗余的打包和解包操作，浪费宝贵的计算资源。

Method: 提出LP-GEMM，一种GEMM核的分解方法，能够在连续GEMM操作之间实现打包布局传播。该方法在保持边界处完整BLAS语义正确性的同时，消除了不必要的数据重打包操作。在x86 (AVX-512) 和RISC-V (RVV 1.0) 架构上，针对MLP类和Attention类工作负载进行了评估。

Result: 在Intel x86平台上，连续GEMM操作相比OpenBLAS平均加速2.25x，相对于Intel MKL等厂商优化库也取得了有竞争力的性能提升。通过实现仅使用BLAS级别GEMM调用的Llama-3.2推理路径，验证了该方法在微基准测试之外的实用性。

Conclusion: 利用操作间的数据布局传播可以显著提升性能，证明了LP-GEMM方法在保持BLAS语义正确性的前提下，能够有效解决连续GEMM操作中的冗余数据移动问题。

Abstract: In Scientific Computing and modern Machine Learning (ML) workloads, sequences of dependent General Matrix Multiplications (GEMMs) often dominate execution time. While state-of-the-art BLAS libraries aggressively optimize individual GEMM calls, they remain constrained by the BLAS API, which requires each call to independently pack input matrices and restore outputs to a canonical memory layout. In sequential GEMMs, these constraints cause redundant packing and unpacking, wasting valuable computational resources.
  This paper introduces LP-GEMM, a decomposition of the GEMM kernel that enables packing-layout propagation across sequential GEMM operations. This approach eliminates unnecessary data repacking while preserving full BLAS semantic correctness at the boundaries. We evaluate LP-GEMM on x86 (AVX-512) and RISC-V (RVV 1.0) architectures across MLP-like and Attention-like workloads. Our results show average speedups of 2.25x over OpenBLAS on Intel x86 for sequential GEMMs and competitive gains relative to vendor-optimized libraries such as Intel MKL.
  We demonstrate the practicality of the approach beyond microbenchmarks by implementing a standalone C++ version of the Llama-3.2 inference path using exclusively BLAS-level GEMM calls. These results confirm that leveraging data layout propagation between operations can significantly boost performance.

</details>


### [267] [Tight Bounds on Window Size and Time for Single-Agent Graph Exploration under T-Interval Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04619)
*Yuichi Sudo,Naoki Kitamura,Masahiro Shibata,Junya Nakamura,Sébastien Tixeuil,Toshimitsu Masuzawa,Koichi Wada*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了T-区间连通动态图中的确定性探索问题，证明了窗口大小T=Ω(m)是必要的，并提出了在广泛边密度范围内紧确的窗口大小界限和探索时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动态网络中单个智能体在未知网络参数（窗口大小T、节点数n、边数m）情况下的确定性探索问题，解决两个基本问题：保证探索的最小窗口大小以及充分大窗口下的最优探索时间。

Method: 针对KT₀（不观察邻居标识符）和KT₁（可观察邻居标识符）两种可见性模型，提出确定性探索算法，算法所需窗口大小为O(ε(n,m)·m + n log²n)，其中ε(n,m) = ln n / (1 + ln m - ln n)。

Result: 证明了窗口大小下界T=Ω(m)；提出的算法在m=n^(1+Θ(1))时达到紧确界限；探索时间方面，KT₀模型下界为Ω((m-n+1)n)，KT₁模型下界为Ω(m)，算法在多对数因子内匹配这些下界；仅用n参数化时，KT₀模型复杂度为Θ(n³)，KT₁模型为Θ(n²)。

Conclusion: 在T-区间连通动态图的探索问题中，本文为两种可见性模型建立了紧确或近乎紧确的窗口大小和探索时间界限，特别是在边密度为m=n^(1+Θ(1))时达到完全最优。

Abstract: We study deterministic exploration by a single agent in $T$-interval-connected graphs, a standard model of dynamic networks in which, for every time window of length $T$, the intersection of the graphs within the window is connected. The agent does not know the window size $T$, nor the number of nodes $n$ or edges $m$, and must visit all nodes of the graph. We consider two visibility models, $KT_0$ and $KT_1$, depending on whether the agent can observe the identifiers of neighboring nodes. We investigate two fundamental questions: the minimum window size that guarantees exploration, and the optimal exploration time under sufficiently large window size.
  For both models, we show that a window size $T = Ω(m)$ is necessary. We also present deterministic algorithms whose required window size is $O(ε(n,m)\cdot m + n \log^2 n)$, where $ε(n,m) = \frac{\ln n}{1 + \ln m - \ln n}$. These bounds are tight for a wide range of $m$, in particular when $m = n^{1+Θ(1)}$. The same algorithms also yield optimal or near-optimal exploration time: we prove lower bounds of $Ω((m - n + 1)n)$ in the $KT_0$ model and $Ω(m)$ in the $KT_1$ model, and show that our algorithms match these bounds up to a polylogarithmic factor, while being fully time-optimal when $m = n^{1+Θ(1)}$. This yields tight bounds when parameterized solely by $n$: $Θ(n^3)$ for $KT_0$ and $Θ(n^2)$ for $KT_1$.

</details>


### [268] [Edge-Oriented Orchestration of Energy Services Using Graph-Driven Swarm Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04645)
*Liana Toderean,Dragos Lazea,Vasile Ofrim,Stefania Dumbrava,Anca Hangan,Tudor Cioara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向智能电网的边-雾-云统一框架，通过图数据模型和群智能启发式算法实现了资源感知与时延敏感的任务卸载，并利用区块链与能源数据空间技术保障了可追溯性与互操作性，在真实环境中验证了零停机服务迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能电网对物联网设备和分布式能源管理的依赖日益加深，系统亟需一种去中心化且低延迟的能源服务编排机制，以应对传统中心化架构在实时性、可扩展性和可靠性方面的不足。

Method: 提出一个适用于智能能源系统的边-雾-云统一框架。首先，采用基于图的数据模型对基础设施和工作负载进行建模，以支持高效的拓扑探索与任务放置；其次，设计一种基于群智能的启发式算法，在资源感知与时延敏感的前提下进行任务卸载；最后，通过符合能源数据空间标准确保数据互操作性，并利用区块链技术对工作负载进行公证，以保障流程的可追溯性。

Result: 在基于KubeEdge的真实部署环境中验证了该框架的有效性。实验表明，该方案能够在动态工作负载下实现零停机的服务迁移，并有效维持服务连续性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了智能电网环境下服务编排的去中心化与低延迟需求，通过理论模型、算法优化与区块链技术的结合，为智能能源系统提供了一种高效、可靠且可追溯的解决方案。

Abstract: As smart grids increasingly depend on IoT devices and distributed energy management, they require decentralized, low latency orchestration of energy services. We address this with a unified framework for edge fog cloud infrastructures tailored to smart energy systems. It features a graph based data model that captures infrastructure and workload, enabling efficient topology exploration and task placement. Leveraging this model, a swarm-based heuristic algorithm handles task offloading in a resource-aware, latency sensitive manner. Our framework ensures data interoperability via energy data space compliance and guarantees traceability using blockchain based workload notarization. We validate our approach with a real-world KubeEdge deployment, demonstrating zero downtime service migration under dynamic workloads while maintaining service continuity.

</details>


### [269] [Communication-Efficient Collaborative LLM Inference over LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04654)
*Songge Zhang,Wen Wu,Liang Li,Ye Wang,Xuemin,Shen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向低地球轨道(LEO)卫星网络的通信高效协作式大语言模型推理方案，通过模型分割、流水线并行和自适应激活压缩技术，显著降低了推理延迟和通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低地球轨道卫星在智能地球观测中扮演重要角色，但由于单颗卫星的星载内存有限且推理延迟过高，大语言模型难以在单星上实际部署。

Method: 将整个大语言模型分割成多个子模型并分别部署在不同卫星上，通过交换中间激活实现协作推理；利用流水线并行机制重叠子模型推理与传输；设计自适应激活压缩方案以减少累积误差；将推理延迟最小化问题转化为有向无环图上的最短路径搜索问题，并采用改进的A*算法求解。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与最先进的基准方案相比，该方案推理延迟降低了42%，通信开销降低了71%，同时保持了推理精度损失小于1%。

Conclusion: 所提出的协作推理方案能够有效解决LEO卫星网络中LLM部署的资源和延迟限制问题，在保证精度的前提下显著提升了推理效率。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites play an essential role in intelligent Earth observation by leveraging artificial intelligence models. However, limited onboard memory and excessive inference delay prevent the practical deployment of large language models (LLMs) on a single satellite. In this paper, we propose a communication-efficient collaborative LLM inference scheme for LEO satellite networks. Specifically, the entire LLM is split into multiple sub-models, with each deployed on a satellite, thereby enabling collaborative LLM inference via exchanging intermediate activations between satellites. The proposed scheme also leverages the pipeline parallelism mechanism that overlaps sub-model inference with intermediate activation transmission, thereby reducing LLM inference delay. An adaptive activation compression scheme is designed to mitigate cumulative errors from multi-stage model splitting while preserving inference accuracy. Furthermore, we formulate the LLM inference delay minimization problem by jointly optimizing model splitting and compression ratios under onboard memory and inference accuracy constraints. The problem is transformed into a shortest-path search problem over a directed acyclic graph that edge weights explicitly quantify the inference delay induced by model splitting and compression strategies, which is solved via a modified A Star-based search algorithm. Extensive simulation results indicate that the proposed solution can reduce inference delay by up to 42% and communication overhead by up to 71% compared to state-of-the-art benchmarks, while maintaining the inference accuracy loss of less than 1%.

</details>


### [270] [Towards Policy-Enabled Multi-Hop Routing for Cross-Chain Message Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04890)
*Amin Rezaei,Solomon L. Davidson,Bernard Wong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了xRoute，一个受传统网络启发的跨链路由与消息传递框架，旨在解决区块链生态系统中的流动性碎片化问题，通过去中心化中继网络提供比现有中心化方案更好的连接性、去中心化程度和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链生态系统面临严重的流动性碎片化问题，应用和资产分散在多条公链上且仅对部分用户可访问。现有的跨链通信方案存在局限性：要么仅支持直连链间通信，要么依赖枢纽（Hub）进行路由，这会导致瓶颈和中心化风险。

Method: 提出xRoute框架，借鉴传统网络设计理念，引入路由、名称解析和基于策略的消息投递机制。该框架允许应用指定路由策略，使目标链能够验证所选路由是否满足安全要求，并采用去中心化的中继网络来计算路由和传递消息，从而避免引入可信枢纽节点。

Result: 在支持跨链通信（IBC）协议的区块链上进行的实验表明，相比基于枢纽的设计，该方法在连接性、去中心化程度和可扩展性方面均有提升，特别是在高负载场景下表现更优。

Conclusion: xRoute成功将传统网络的路由机制适配到区块链环境，有效解决了现有跨链方案的中心化和扩展性瓶颈，为解决区块链流动性碎片化问题提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Blockchain ecosystems face a significant issue with liquidity fragmentation, as applications and assets are distributed across many public chains with each only accessible by subset of users. Cross-chain communication was designed to address this by allowing chains to interoperate, but existing solutions limit communication to directly connected chains or route traffic through hubs that create bottlenecks and centralization risks.
  In this paper, we introduce xRoute, a cross-chain routing and message-delivery framework inspired by traditional networks. Our design brings routing, name resolution, and policy-based delivery to the blockchain setting. It allows applications to specify routing policies, enables destination chains to verify that selected routes satisfy security requirements, and uses a decentralized relayer network to compute routes and deliver messages without introducing a trusted hub. Experiments on the chains supporting the Inter-Blockchain Communication (IBC) protocol show that our approach improves connectivity, decentralization, and scalability compared to hub-based designs, particularly under heavy load.

</details>


### [271] [The Energy Cost of Execution-Idle in GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04745)
*Yiran Lei,Jared Fernandez,Vasilis Kypriotis,Dimitrios Skarlatos,Emma Strubell,Justine Sherry,Daniel Vosler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文识别并表征了GPU在数据中心中一种被忽视的高功耗低活跃状态——执行空闲（execution-idle），该状态消耗了约10.7%的能源；通过构建两种原型系统，展示了通过自动降频和负载均衡优化该状态的可行性与必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据中心中，GPU正成为主要的功耗来源。与CPU不同，GPU在可见活动接近零时仍可能维持高功耗状态（即执行空闲状态）。现有研究缺乏对该状态在实际部署中普遍性和影响的深入理解，因此需要对其进行分析并提出优化方案。

Method: 作者利用来自大型学术AI集群的每秒级遥测数据，对执行空闲状态进行了详细表征。基于此发现，构建了两个原型系统进行验证：一个系统在执行空闲期间自动降低GPU频率（downscaling），另一个系统通过调整负载不均衡来减少暴露在该状态下的时间。

Result: 在多种工作负载和多个GPU代际中，执行空闲状态占据了执行时间的19.7%和总能耗的10.7%。原型实验表明，自动降频和负载均衡策略能有效应对该状态，但均存在一定的性能权衡。

Conclusion: 执行空闲是GPU实际部署中普遍存在且能耗显著的状态。未来的GPU能效系统设计应将执行空闲视为一种一等公民操作状态，并针对性地开发管理策略以降低其成本和暴露时间。

Abstract: GPUs are becoming a major contributor to data center power, yet unlike CPUs, they can remain at high power even when visible activity is near zero. We call this state execution-idle. Using per-second telemetry from a large academic AI cluster, we characterize execution-idle as a recurring low-activity yet high-power state in real deployments. Across diverse workloads and multiple GPU generations, it accounts for 19.7% of in-execution time and 10.7% of energy. This suggests a need to both reduce the cost of execution-idle and reduce exposure to it. We therefore build two prototypes: one uses automatic downscaling during execution-idle, and the other uses load imbalance to reduce exposure, both with performance trade-offs. These findings suggest that future energy-efficient GPU systems should treat execution-idle as a first-class operating state.

</details>
